# Clew · handover for the first build session

> Session history. For current integration instructions start with [docs/README.md](docs/README.md); [implementation status](docs/reference/status.md) distinguishes delivered behavior from proposals. Older entries retain the terminology and state of their session.

Written 2026-11-03 at the end of the design exploration in the `startsidan` repository. Clew is the codename for a new, separate POC. The product name in the Swedish UI stays **Startsidan**.

## 0. State after session 1 (2026-11-03)

Everything in the first-session checklist is done and verified. Read `README.md` for the run commands.

What exists and works:

- Devcontainer: `node:26.13.0-bookworm` (Node 26 LTS since 2026-10-28; was 24.21.0 until session 7) + Bun 1.4.4, Postgres 17, optional `calendar` profile with Nextcloud 33 + RoomVox + Mailpit (ported from startsidan, NATS removed, no Temporal). Only `127.0.0.1:3020` (Vite) is published; Vite proxies `/api` to the Hono BFF on container port 3000, so browser, cookies and the OIDC callback share one origin.
- Keycloak: dedicated realm `clew`, client `clew-web`, users `hillevi` (manager, rektor) and `hassan` (employee). `bun run auth:setup` is idempotent; the shared local Keycloak container is `keycloak` (start it with `docker start keycloak` if `keycloak.orb.local:8090` does not answer).
- Auth: `packages/platform/src/auth.ts` from startsidan ported to `apps/server/src/auth.ts` (openid-client 6.8.9, jose 6.2.15). Real login verified in Chrome and in `scripts/auth-dev-check.ts`.
- Drizzle: `auth_*` tables, `thread`, `thread_message`, `proposal`, `audit`, `job`. Migration `0000` checked in under `packages/db/migrations`.
- Calendar: `packages/calendar` holds the model, Stockholm date helpers, the Nextcloud CalDAV provider (ported, plus a `freeBusy()` that returns busy periods per attendee), synthetic account bindings and the deterministic planner `planWeek()`.
- Server (`apps/server`, Hono on Node via tsx): auth routes, threads API, question router, planner (push, replan, confirm, undo, undo-expiry, dismiss), figures, today view, attention counter, Postgres-backed scheduler.
- Web (`apps/web`, Vite + React 19 + Astryx 0.7.7 Neutral), rebuilt 2026-11-03 against the design canvas with Astryx components only: `ChatComposer` question box with suggestion chips, dictation and a "Menar du / Fortsätt tråd" list; `ClickableCard` + `StatusDot` + `ProgressBar` Läget tiles; `List` for I dag with gaps as rows; thread anatomy as on the canvas (badge and source, one sentence, assessment, `Grid` week agenda, actions, "Varför nu?", `ChatComposer` reply, older turns behind a `Collapsible`); `Collapsible` + `List` for "Dina trådar och redan hanterat"; calendar page as `Layout` with an end `LayoutPanel` (`CheckboxList`, confirm, "Föreslå andra tider", reply); mobile with a `BottomSheet` composer and a `CheckboxList` thread. Astryx renders on the plain CSS path; **no StyleX plugin is needed** as long as nobody writes `stylex.create()` in app code.
- Acceptance verified end to end on 2026-11-03 with `bun scripts/auth-dev-check.ts hillevi` (run on the host): login as Hillevi, four proposals in week 46 that collide with nothing in her or the four employees' calendars, confirm creates four CalDAV events and one audit row, undo within ten minutes removes them and writes a second audit row, the thread survives reload. Unit tests cover the planner and free-gap logic (`bun test tests/unit`).

Session 4 (2026-11-03, evening) built the module architecture so it can be tested with mock integrations:

- `packages/modules`: the capability layer (D1, D3). `Module` = id, kind (core, passthrough, derived), source label, areas, capabilities, optional `scan`. `Capability` = name, kind (read, propose, execute), Swedish title and description, TypeBox input/output, `model` flag, `scope(actor)`, `run`. `Registry` registers modules, runs capabilities with scope checked on every call, derives areas per role, scans for thread drafts, and lists tools (never execute, D8).
- Mock integrations in `packages/modules/src/mock`: Personalsystemet (`hr`: absences, leave, delegations, shifts), Ekonomisystemet (`finance`: invoices, budget), Avvikelsesystemet (`incidents`: deviations), Kommunikation (notices with read receipts). Their records live in `packages/modules/src/mock/data.ts`; mutations persist in the `module_state` table through `@clew/db/state` (in-memory store for unit tests). One invoice answers "unknown" on purpose.
- Core modules with no adapter: `apps/server/src/people-module.ts` (users and permissions from `auth_person` and `auth_access_grant`; every other module's `scope` reads role and unit from here, the mocks link records by `personId`). The calendar is wrapped as a module in `apps/server/src/calendar-module.ts`; proposals keep the planner path.
- Threads are generic cards now: `module`, `area`, `summary`, `items` (rows with refs), `actions` (thread action contract: one primary, up to two secondary, overflow), `waitingOn` (Pågår utan dig), `deadline`, `receipt` (read-back rows: done, unknown, failed, skipped, undo window). Migrations 0001 and 0002; a partial unique index keeps one open pushed thread per person and topic, and the scan is serialised per person.
- API: `GET /api/areas`, `GET /api/modules` (the registry as data), `GET|POST /api/cap/:name` (standing actions, panel views), `POST /api/threads/:id/act|undo-receipt|snooze|dismiss`. The nightly job scans every person, not only managers; `receipt-final` closes undo windows.
- Web: `pages/start.tsx` is mission control (areas sidebar from Astryx List rows, Behöver dig sorted by deadline, Pågår utan dig collapsed, composer docked in the footer, Drawer as phase-one side panel). `components/card.tsx` renders any module's card with slots, ⋯ menu (Inte nu, Avfärda, Varför ser jag detta?) and the receipt.
- Checks: `bun test tests/unit` (13 tests, registry and mocks), `bun scripts/modules-check.ts` on the host (real login as Hillevi and Hassan, areas, cards, act, receipt, undo, unknown outcome, snooze, read receipt, scope denial).

Session 5 (2026-11-03, evening) reviewed the build against the canvas rows Fokus, Fokus 3, Chips, Kort-handlingar and Svar 4, then built the conversation:

- Server: `apps/server/src/assistant/` replaces `assistant.ts`. `POST /api/conversation {text, chips?, conversationId?}` answers in one of three shapes (`svar`, `vy`, `forslag`) and says how (`model`, `router`, `unavailable`). Order: the **chip path** first (one chipped card: `parseVerb` → Avfärda/Inte nu become `clewActions` to confirm, Varför answers from the trigger, "bara Anna och Johan" writes a draft to `items[].selected` with audit `thread.drafted`, "vecka 47 i stället" calls `planner.replan`), then the **model** (`generateText` with `registry.tools(actor)` as read-only tools, names mapped `heroma.whoIsOn` ↔ `heroma__whoIsOn`, 8 s timeout, Swedish system prompt with a "Du tittar på" block), then the **router** (the old fixed questions, then `matchCapability` over titles and descriptions, then an honest "not yet"). Every turn is a `question` thread with memory; `thread_message.meta` (migration 0003) keeps chips, refs, shape, view, draft, tool calls and mode. Also `GET /api/completions` (ask/do/recent per role), `GET /api/objects?q=` (visible objects first, then capabilities), `GET /api/threads/:id/verbs`, `GET /api/assistant/status` (configured/available, 60 s probe), `POST /api/threads/:id/reopen` (Ångra after Avfärda within 10 min, or after Inte nu). Proposal threads now carry `items` (`proposal:<id>`, names in rows per the decision below).
- Web: `components/composer.tsx` is the row (Astryx `ChatComposer`): the focus list (Fråga om verksamheten · Om det som visas · Gör något · Tidigare samtal) and the conversation live in the composer's **drawer** slot, unfolding upward from the row; the stream is dimmed with Astryx `Overlay` (D13); chips are tokens in the field via `@` (`ChatComposerInput` trigger over `/api/objects`) or "Lägg till i samtalet" on a card; the prefix reads Allt / Om 1 sak / Samtalet; with one chip and no text the footer shows the verbs; Esc or a click on the dim closes and the conversation reopens from Tidigare samtal or Redan hanterat › Frågor. Cards share the action row (primary, ≤2 secondary, Lägg till i samtalet, Inte nu = snooze, ⋯ with Avfärda and Varför ser jag detta?); Avfärda and Inte nu leave a toast with Ångra (reopen); the receipt badge says "Skickat hh:mm · kontrollerat mot …" and the primary slot becomes "Ångra allt · m:ss kvar". Redan hanterat is grouped by day with filters, Återöppna and reopening of questions; the mobile area row has a Redan hanterat button.
- Checks: `bun test tests/unit` (23 tests, including a real AI SDK wiring test with a fake fetch), `bun scripts/modules-check.ts` (conversation through the router, chips, drafts, reopen, objects, completions, verbs, employee scope), and the flows above verified in Chrome as Hillevi at desktop width. Mobile width was not screenshot this session (the automation window would not shrink); the components are the same.
- Astryx CLI under Bun isolated installs: `bun run astryx` cannot find core from the repo root. Working form: `docker exec -w /workspace/node_modules/.bun/@astryxdesign+core@0.7.6+65f46399a9db101b/node_modules/@astryxdesign/core clew-app-1 node /workspace/node_modules/@astryxdesign/cli/clients/cli/bin/astryx.mjs component <Name>` (see README).
- Routes (react-router through Astryx `LinkProvider`, so every Astryx `href` navigates client-side): `/` and `/areas/:area` filter the stream, `/handled` is the log, `/views/:capability?title=&input=` and `/views/card/:threadId/:actionId` open the side panel (back or Esc closes it), `/conversations/:id` opens a conversation in the row (back closes it). `?area=` keeps the lens behind a view or conversation. The sidebar rows, the mobile area buttons, Redan hanterat's question rows and Tidigare samtal are links.
- Decided with the product owner: names may appear in card rows (the canvas wins over HANDOVER item 3; the guardrail now covers pushed text and notifications). The D8 degraded-mode banner is deferred; the status endpoint exists for it.

Still open: add a provider and an open-weight model under Administration › Modeller (D24 replaced the `AI_*` variables in session 29) and verify live; `bun scripts/models-check.ts --url … --model …` proves the adapters against it first (so far only a stand-in endpoint and the router have answered); build the D8 banner from `GET /api/assistant/status`; a `propose` capability so "flytta Annas till 15:00" can become a draft.

Still open: settle how a delegated Graph token reaches the BFF (Keycloak as broker in front of Entra with stored external tokens, or OIDC directly against Entra in production) and run the contract against a real tenant. Needs an Entra tenant. Reword D5 so the municipality's IdP is the production authorization server and Keycloak is dev and broker.

Left: drag an object to the row; `event:` and `proposal:` chips are resolved server-side but the side panel's day view that would offer them is not built; the ScrollableArea in the drawer has a fixed height (56vh desktop, 360 px mobile) because Astryx has no max-height prop, so short conversations leave space above; reminders ("påminn mig om två veckor") need a `reminder` job kind.

Until then a connector runs inside the app container (README, "Connectors").

Identity, 2026-11-09 (D62, D68, D85 built; `docs/architecture/identity.md` is current): still open, in the order they matter: the person-id question (the seed creates `hillevi`, `hassan`, `mikael` with those ids because the calendar accounts and the HR fixture's actor map key on them; a real provisioner's people get server ids, so either those move to `userName` on the authenticated context or the seed keeps creating the rows), protocol 2's `Actor` without `externalId` or `userName` for a connector to map a person with, `OIDC_BINDING_CLAIM` for the production IdP, Metakatalogen's API and stable id, storing `protected`, an organisation scope on groups, a per-row gate under Administration for a person with only one admin capability, and the browser mock's missing `/chat-services` route (two `admin.spec.ts` cases). ## 1. What Clew is

A chat-first start page for Sundsvalls kommun where the assistant takes the initiative. There is no navigation, no tabs and no configurable blocks. The question box is the page. Everything else on the page is the assistant answering "what needs me now?" as **threads** the user can reply to in place. The assistant pushes threads on a schedule or on events, rations its interruptions, and executes actions through MCP tools after the user confirms.

Design canvas (Claude Design, private until shared): https://claude.ai/artifact/RhnCjPeZZHMmbvHQ5ZXobe

The start page as built since session 27 follows the canvas **Clew startsida · översikt i stället för en lista** (Claude Design, https://claude.ai/artifact/UiCMmm2fmEaq1M7cipBDvQ, rows four to six: the box with the brief, the conversation, the rail, the page without the model), decided in D34; review `docs/reviews/2026-11-04-start-page-overview-exploration.md`.

The start page as built since 2026-11-06 is Hem (D53), artboard A of the canvas **Clew startsida · dagen i ett svep** (Claude Design, https://claude.ai/artifact/9xn43bAwXy2VioBqiG3xc6, private until shared): four directions A to D, then the flows E to K under A (Granska beside the column, asking about the card, a question from Hem, an older conversation, a view as a page, work across three connectors, the phone's sheet); review `docs/reviews/2026-11-06-start-page-directions-and-flows.md`.

The visual hierarchy, the area mark and the figure forms are proposed on the canvas **Clew · hierarki, områden och figurer** (Claude Design, https://claude.ai/artifact/YVw6FJL6YjKZprBAiAEA2f, private until shared): five spec artboards (three type levels, the area mark, the figure's three forms, the answer with its embedded view, the row and the open card), not built, awaiting the product owner's decision; review `docs/reviews/2026-11-06-visual-hierarchy-area-marks-and-figures.md`.

The workspace structure (the rail, Att göra, the panel beside the column, the box on every page) follows the canvas **Clew · ett sammanhängande flöde** (Claude Design, https://claude.ai/artifact/1dDLtj7R5AMmUa4qZNk6Da, private until shared): a flow map, Hem, Samtal med panel, Att göra, Verktygssida and two phone frames, decided in D50; review `docs/reviews/2026-11-05-ux-flow-exploration.md`.

How a source attaches to an entity (Personal without per-row feature buttons, the lens under Visa, the opened person as sections, the map of every surface, the connector developer's side) is drawn on the canvas **Clew · en entitet, många källor** (Claude Design, https://claude.ai/artifact/Muchzp6aRAx9uxokwFLAfj): 19 artboards, written but not rendered and checked, the direction in D97, nothing built; review `docs/reviews/2026-11-10-one-entity-many-sources.md`.

Rows on the first canvas, top to bottom:

1. Chat-only start page, desktop (Hillevi, rektor) and mobile (Hassan, undersköterska), thread anatomy, target architecture, three sticky notes.
2. Asking a question on mobile: bottom sheet for the moment, thread on the page for the memory.
3. Asking a question on desktop: typeahead under the box, answer unfolds as a thread.
4. Hybrid: chat-first page plus a persistent "Läget" row with four key figures.
5. **Use case to build first:** a manager plans utvecklingssamtal, with proposals drawn in a week view, a full calendar page and a mobile version.

Source material: `~/Desktop/SC_Ekonomi 261103.pdf` (the steering deck, 14 slides) and the `startsidan` repository at `~/code/startsidan`.

## 2. Decisions taken

| Area | Decision | Note |
|---|---|---|
| Codename | `clew` (a ball of thread; Ariadne's clew leads out of the labyrinth) | Repo `clew`, packages `@clew/*`. UI says Startsidan. |
| Dev environment | Devcontainer, Docker Compose, same shape as startsidan | `.devcontainer/` copied from startsidan: `node:26.13.0-bookworm` (Node 26 LTS, updated 2026-11-04), Bun 1.4.4, Postgres 17, Nextcloud calendar profile. NATS removed. `.mise.toml` matches the Dockerfile. Commands run through `docker exec clew-app-1`. |
| Package manager and scripts | Bun, exact versions, frozen lockfile | Bun 1.4 uses isolated installs: every workspace package declares what it imports (no hoisting to rely on). |
| Server runtime | **Node via `tsx`**, decided 2026-11-04, D16 (supersedes D7) | The Temporal worker runs on Node only. Bun stays as package manager, script runner and unit test runner. Hono serves through `@hono/node-server`. |
| Backend | **Hono 4.13.16 + `@hono/node-server` 2.1.6** | Decided 2026-11-03. Web-standard `Request`/`Response`; AI SDK and MCP plug in without adapters. Note: Hono routes handler errors to `app.onError`, not through middleware `try/catch`, so the error mapper lives in `onError`. |
| Frontend | React 19.3.1 + Vite 8.3.5 + `@astryxdesign/core` 0.7.12 (upgraded from 0.7.7 on 2026-11-10, `docs/reviews/2026-11-10-astryx-0.7.12-upgrade.md`) with the default Neutral theme (`/built` + `theme.css`) | Plain CSS path, no StyleX plugin. `astryx doctor` is green. Swedish strings from `locales/sv-SE.json` plus a few overrides in `packages/ui`. React Router 8.5.1 for the three routes; TanStack Query 5.105.3 for every read the browser makes (D33). |
| UI composition | **Strictly Astryx, no custom components.** Decided 2026-11-03 after the design review. The week view is an Astryx `Grid` agenda (one column per weekday, `Token`/`ListItem` entries), not a time grid. `styles.css` holds only the Astryx imports and body basics. | The startsidan week-grid exception is withdrawn. AI surfaces use Astryx's Chat family; Vercel's `ai-elements` (shadcn/Tailwind/Radix) was evaluated and rejected as a second component system. Use it only as a pattern reference. |
| Database | Postgres 17.11 + Drizzle 0.45.4 + drizzle-kit 0.31.11 | `bun run db:generate`, review SQL, `bun run db:init` applies. Migrations are checked in. |
| Identity | Keycloak OIDC for people, OAuth 2.1 JWT access tokens for every call on their behalf, per the MCP authorization spec 2026-07-28 plus the Enterprise-Managed Authorization extension (ID-JAG). **Only identification path.** | Ported login, not rewritten. Realm `clew`, client `clew-web`. Session cookie `clew-session`, 8 h idle and absolute. No per-server consent popup: token exchange inside our Keycloak, ID-JAG across trust domains, `authorization_code` only as logged fallback. The model has no identity. SCIM 2.0 provisions users and groups. Keycloak accepts ID-JAGs from 26.7 (experimental); dev runs 26.2. Design: `docs/architecture/identity.md`, D5. |
| Model dependency | **The model is optional.** Every read, propose and execute path is deterministic and reachable without it; the composer degrades to an intent router with typeahead. | Decided 2026-11-03, D8. The planner and the decision endpoint already work this way. |
| Side panel | Conversation, Clew-rendered views and sandboxed MCP App views in one panel beside the stream (bottom sheet on phone), with a "Du tittar på" scope block. | Decided 2026-11-03, D9. The MCP App views are replaced by D38 (2026-11-05): a connector declares its view and Clew draws it. Clicking a proposal shows the day around it; "Se i Heroma" shows Heroma's own view. Names may appear in an app surface, never in a card. |
| Interaction pattern | **Objects, chips, proposals.** Every rendered thing has a ref; add it to the conversation as a chip by click, drag or `@`; the conversation edits cards as drafts; the card's button executes. | Decided 2026-11-03, D12. Modelled on Cursor/Claude Code context pills, M365 Copilot Context IQ and Gemini `@Calendar`. |
| Dev login | Username and password form, local only (`AUTH_DEV_LOGIN=true`, never in production) | Mints a normal session for a seeded persona, marked `auth: 'dev'`. Decided 2026-11-03, not built. D6. |
| Integration contracts | **Clew owns every contract.** Capability descriptors (`kind`, `scope`, `model`) in `packages/contracts`; REST views and model tools are projections of the same capability. | No OpenAPI-to-MCP conversion in the running system; Clew publishes OpenAPI for its own BFF instead. Foreign MCP servers are wrapped, not passed through. Design: `docs/architecture/integrations.md`, D1–D4. |
| Who meets the contract | **Integrations are asked to fulfil Clew's API first; if they cannot, Clew writes the adapter.** Decided 2026-11-03, D10. | Sundsvall today: Heroma has no API (RPA), Raindance and aggregates go through dept44 services behind WSO2 (`service` mode + `scope`), Microsoft 365 through Graph (`user-delegated`). |
| Connector kinds | Core (Clew DB), pass-through (external system, refs + audit only), derived (snapshot) | Core has no provider interface. Pass-through is in-process first for Clew's own core, lifted to its own connector service (D21) when cadence, network zone or ownership demands. D3. |
| Backend structure | **A modular monolith with ports and adapters: one folder per module, four kinds of file (contracts, domain, usecases, adapters), thin routes, modules never import each other, checked by `dependency-cruiser` in `bun run check`.** Decided 2026-11-04, D20. | Not tactical DDD. `docs/architecture/backend-structure.md`; rules in `CLAUDE.md`; `bun run deps`. |
| Testing | **Use cases are factories over ports (`model.ts`), paired with live adapters only in `apps/server/src/composition/`; the unit suite runs on memory adapters; a store contract per port runs on both adapters (memory in `bun run check`, Postgres via `bun run test:db`).** Decided 2026-11-04, D25. | `docs/architecture/testing.md` says where a test goes. `bun run check` never needs the stack. Enforced by `usecases-reach-stores-through-ports` (error), `unit-tests-stay-fast` and `bun run purity`. Every `usecases` folder is ported: threads, calendar (`ProposalStore` + `@clew/calendar/memory`), mocks. |
| Connectors | **Every integration, the Clew team's or a third party's, is a connector: its own service behind the Clew connector protocol; Clew loads it from its own `connector` rows, registered by an administrator (D23, built: table, sealed secrets, boot loader, `scripts/connector-add.ts`; admin API and screens open); in-process connectors only for Clew's core.** Decided 2026-11-04, D21; the word decided 2026-11-04, D22 ("module" keeps only D20's code meaning; Swedish "anslutning"). | `docs/architecture/connector-protocol.md`, `docs/protocol/connector-protocol.schema.json`, `connectors/hello`, `scripts/connector-check.ts`, callback `POST /api/connectors/call`, `thread.connector` (migration 0005). Replaces the `apps/mcp-<name>` shape. |
| Connector administration | **Connectors are registered and governed in the database by an administrator: no environment variables, no restart, sealed secrets, an audience per connector enforced in the registry; bundled connectors get rows too.** Decided 2026-11-04, D23, built in sessions 15 and 18. | `docs/architecture/connector-administration.md`; `apps/server/src/connectors/{model,describe,reconcile}.ts`, `usecases/admin.ts`, `adapters/`, `routes/admin.ts`, `apps/web/src/pages/admin/`; grant capability `admin.connectors` (`mikael`). Canvas row for the screens still to draw. |
| Model administration | **Providers and models are added in the product, sealed keys, an audience and a default per model; the composer lets the person choose. No `AI_*` variables.** Accepted 2026-11-04, D24; built 2026-11-05 (session 29). | `docs/architecture/connector-administration.md`, section Modeller. Narrows the vision's Clew/Eneo line to "which endpoints and models, and who may pick them". The turn resolves the person's model from the rows each time (pick, else default, else first; none is the router); the menu sits in `ChatComposer`'s `footerActions`. |
| Scheduled work | **Temporal for durable timers (D17, 2026-11-04); the Postgres `job` table still runs the nightly scan and the undo windows (D15)** | `temporalio/auto-setup` 1.29.7 and `temporalio/ui` 2.58.0 in the devcontainer, SDK 1.25.0, worker in the BFF process, task queue `clew`. First workflow: `reminder` ("Påminn mig om 10 sekunder" on any card). Design and migration path: `docs/architecture/scheduled-work.md`. One `job` table, row-locked claims, nightly `scan-utvecklingssamtal` at 06:00 Stockholm plus one `undo-expiry` timer per confirmation. Temporal comes with the push engine / second use case. |
| Errands | **One connector per case system (`sm`, `casedata`, …), a shared errand profile in the protocol for the reads (`<id>.errands`, `<id>.errand`, `<id>.errandCard`, `<id>.search` with `kinds: ['case']`), each system's own executes, and a core `clew.errands` that combines the lists; a person acts from a card, and a card's buttons reach only its own connector.** D18 proposed 2026-11-04; its hosting (`packages/errands`, `ErrandProvider`, `errand.*`) replaced by D35, accepted 2026-11-05 with only the card-action rule built. | Cards from non-personal fields only (errand number, category, status, what waits); free text read live into the panel. `If-Match` on every execute, receipts read back. Identity to dept44 services as a service credential with asserted `X-Sent-By`, as Draken does, until the gateway accepts exchanged tokens. Review: `docs/reviews/2026-11-04-support-management-integration-review.md`. |
| Change notification | **A Clew-owned contract: payload-free CloudEvents 1.0 over HTTP to one ingest route, signed per source, idempotent, an event triggers a scan or a workflow signal; polling stays the fallback.** Proposed 2026-11-04, D19. | Foreign webhooks (Graph, SupportManagement's channel) are translated by the adapter. Not a broker, not Clew's outbound events yet. |
| Calendar access | **In-process behind the `CalendarProvider` interface, MCP later. One contract, two adapters: `caldav` and `microsoft365`, selected per account.** | Decided 2026-11-03, D11. The interface in `packages/calendar/src/model.ts` is the tool surface an MCP server would expose; `packages/calendar/src/adapters.ts` picks the adapter from `CalendarCredential.kind`. `caldav` is the verified Nextcloud provider; `microsoft365` is a typed branch that throws until built. `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` is not installed yet. |
| Agent actions | Confirm-first in version 1 | The human decision endpoint (`POST /api/threads/:id/confirm`) re-validates every proposal against live calendars and is not a tool. Undo is ten minutes. |
| Language | Swedish only in the product UI (and API errors the UI shows). English for code, comments, console and script output, infra messages, docs and commit messages (commit subjects were Swedish until 2026-11-05). | Tightened 2026-11-03: scripts and console output used to be Swedish. |
| Clew and Eneo | **Clew stands alone; Eneo enhances it through an optional `eneo` connector.** Decided 2026-11-04, D14. | Clew owns the AI that manipulates Clew; Eneo owns the AI that is a product in itself. Nothing in the repo depends on Eneo. Who-does-what table in `docs/vision.md`. |
| The stream | **Two groups (Nu, Senare) and three piles (Pågår utan dig, Inte nu, Klart i dag) above the composer; the day drawn as one track on the digest card; snooze as its own column `snoozed_until`; the reason as a Token.** Accepted 2026-11-04, D28, built in session 21. | `streamGroup`, `dayWord`, `isSnoozed`, `dayRhythm`, `stockholmInstant` and `nextStockholmTime` in `@clew/contracts`; `rhythmFor` in `threads/domain.ts`; `GET /api/attention` carries `rhythm`; routes `/waiting`, `/snoozed`, `/handled`. A snoozed card wakes when its time passes (sweep before every read and on the scan) or when the scan changes its draft, audited as `thread.woken`. |
| Inte nu | **A timed snooze with the resolved time shown, free text in the composer's grammar, the D17 reminder workflow, broken by activity; conditions ("bevaka tills") deferred.** Accepted 2026-11-04, D29, built in session 21. Since D49 it comes back the way a reminder does, first under Nu with the badge and the toast, and it is the one time action on an open card. | `snoozePresets`, `parseSwedishTime`, `snoozeWhen`, `SnoozeInput` in `@clew/contracts` (shared by server and web); `POST /api/threads/:id/snooze { preset | text | until }`, `GET /api/snooze/presets`; the threads use cases start a silent `reminder` workflow per snooze (`snooze:<thread>:<ms>`) and cancel it on wake; `TimePicker` in `card.tsx` is one Astryx DropdownMenu (adaptive) with the resolved time per row plus a Dialog for free text, used by both Inte nu and Påminn mig. The reminder cap is 30 days. |
| Proposal threads | **An obligation with four steps (Förslag, Inbjudningar, Svar, Klart), replies read back from the calendar, one Temporal follow-up workflow per obligation under a fixed policy, judgement calls as rows with reasons, load per weekday from the loaded week, "annan tid" as two alternatives on the same thread.** Accepted 2026-11-04, D30, built in session 21; the live Temporal run and Nextcloud's PARTSTAT path are not yet verified. | `ProposalStatus` gains `accepted`, `declined-by-invitee`, `counter`; `proposal.reminders` and `reminded_at` (migration `0009`); `obligationStep`, `replySummary` in the contracts; `unplacedReasons`, `dayLoad`, `addWorkdays` in `@clew/calendar`; `usecases/obligation.ts` holds the policy (`FOLLOW_UP`, `obligationPlan`, `nextReminderAt`, `returnAt`, `nextCheck`); `readReplies`, `remindInvitees`, `obligationCheck`, `returnObligation` in `samtal.ts`; the `obligation` workflow and three activities in `temporal/`; `temporal/obligations.ts` is the timers adapter (beside the client, because a module never imports the platform); `composition/calendar.ts` exports a `notifier` slot that `index.ts` fills with `threads.pushDraft`. After confirm the thread is open and waiting on "svar från N medarbetare" (the Pågår utan dig pile), not handled. |
| The calendar family and a person's own bookings | **One calendar family: `EventCalendar` on the page and, compact, as the `DayStrip` and `EventList` embeds (side panel day view, event dialog, the proposals' week in the transcript), Astryx `Calendar` as the date picker, `agendaItems()` as the one mapping; Dag / Vecka / Månad addressable as `?date=&view=`; a booking, a move, a cancellation and a moved proposal are use cases with audit rows behind thin routes, never tools; every action has a dialog as its keyboard path; a phone gets FullCalendar's list and month.** Accepted 2026-11-04, D32, built in session 23. | `packages/ui/src/{calendar,calendar-embeds,agenda-items}.tsx`, `apps/server/src/modules/calendar/usecases/events.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/calendar/{event-dialog,booking-dialog}.tsx`, `pages/calendar.tsx`, `tests/a11y/calendar.spec.ts`, `tests/unit/events.test.ts`. Review: `docs/reviews/2026-11-04-calendar-against-the-canvas.md`. |
| The shell and the conversation | **One AppShell for start, calendar and administration; the start page is Layout contentWidth 960 → ChatLayout with the composer in its slot and the piles in the column's toolbar; a conversation swaps the stream for the transcript; chips are Tokens in ChatComposerDrawer; no Overlay, no project CSS; the one theme override is scroll padding on chat-layout.** Accepted 2026-11-04, D31, supersedes D13. | Plan: `docs/reviews/2026-11-04-astryx-adoption-against-the-templates.md`; built step by step on branch `astryx-migration`. |
| Questions from documents | **A search fan-out with citations, not a knowledge module: one `SearchHit` contract, one composite `clew.search` tool for the model, the router's catch-all without a model, a `document` chip, a card-from-answer use case, Eneo as one source.** Proposed 2026-11-04, D26; built in full on branch `worktree-fraga-d26` (session 25). | Clew indexes nothing and stores refs only. The fan-out is parallel with a per-source and a total budget; a source is connected by registering a connector with a `<id>.search` capability, and a source without its own search brings its index in its container. Settled with the product owner: follow-ups from the model with headings as fallback, staleness from the source's interval with 12 months as fallback, "Fråga ägaren" left out, "Steg" as a third answer shape for procedures, and the Eneo connector acts with the person's token (RFC 8693 token exchange, `delegation` in the manifest, `X-Clew-Delegated-Token`). Canvas row 3 (I to L). `connectors/eneo` is built against recorded answers and the dev Keycloak, not yet against a real Eneo. |
| Calendar page | **FullCalendar 7 Standard (MIT) through `@fullcalendar/react`, themed with Astryx tokens, Astryx components around and inside it; `WeekAgenda` stays for the start-page card.** Decided 2026-11-04, D27. | `docs/reviews/2026-11-04-calendar-component-alternatives.md`. Only Standard packages, no licence key; drag for proposals only with a keyboard path; list or day view on phones. Theme and week view started in session 19. |
| Browser reads | **One TanStack Query cache per confirmed session, mounted under the SessionBoundary and cleared when it ends; every read in `queries.ts` with its key in `query-keys.ts`; a mutation's thread written through `applyThread`; refetch on tab visibility and reconnect, no polling; a failed background refetch shows no alert.** Accepted 2026-11-04, D33, built in session 24 (worktree `tanstack-query`, branch `tanstack-query`, not merged). | `@tanstack/react-query` 5.105.3 MIT, devtools in development only. Retires `clew:refresh`, the page `load()` functions, `presetCache` in `card.tsx` and the `version` counter on the connector page. |
| Start page | **The box is the page (D34), drawn as Astryx's `ai-chat-landing` template (D41).** `/` is "Hej, <first name>" and "Vad vill du göra?" over a three-line box, with a brief of at most three rows above it and the person's areas as toggles under it, the chosen area's standing actions as cards (an area may name an icon from Clew's list); `/overview` is the whole picture (Läget, Nu, Senare, Jag föreslår, Till kännedom beside the day and the piles); one rail ordered by time (Från mig, Pågår, Samtal, then Kalender, Områden, Administration); four kinds derived by `cardKind`; Neutral with a municipal blue accent. | `docs/decisions/D34-the-box-is-the-page-and-the-push-is-its-brief.md`, `docs/decisions/D41-the-landing-is-the-ai-chat-landing-template-with-the-persons-areas-under-the-box.md` |
| Views | **A view is declared by the connector and drawn by Clew (D38).** A read capability may carry a `view` in its manifest: `table`, `list`, `detail` or `text`, fields with a Swedish label and a format (`phone`, `email`, `link`, `date`, `instant`, `number`, `status`), a search field and a choice bound to the capability's own input, and the read of the same connector a row opens. No markup, no styling, no iframe: the MCP App views of D9 are replaced. One renderer (`apps/web/src/components/view.tsx`, Astryx only) draws it in the side panel and, compact, under the latest answer. `modelReads: 'summary'` keeps the content from the model and the stored conversation. | Accepted and built 2026-11-05 (session 33). `ViewSchema` in `packages/contracts`, rules in `packages/connectors/src/views.ts`, `connectors/telefonbok` as the reference, `docs/architecture/connector-protocol.md` "Declaring a view". Not built: `figures`, `series`, `sections`, a row that proposes a card, a full-page view. A view in the menu is D40 (the row below). |
| Menu tools | **A connector's views can be tools in the menu, and an administrator chooses which (D40).** Any read capability that declares a view and whose input lists nothing as required can be a tool (`menuEligible` in `packages/connectors/src/views.ts`). A capability may name its row with `menu: { label, icon }` (`MenuHintSchema`; an icon name from Clew's list; refused by `parseManifest` and `Registry.register` on a capability that cannot be a tool); without it the row takes the capability's title and the `document` icon (`menuRowOf`). The administrator chooses view by view under Områden on the connector's page ("Verktyg i menyn", a `List` with a `Switch` each): the admin use case `update` with `menu: { "<capability>": true | false }` stores `menu: true` in the capability's override (`connector.capabilities`) and writes one audit row `connector.menu` per view. `Registry.menu(actor)` gives the rows a person gets (audience, connector on, view chosen, read allowed, still a tool), `GET /api/areas` hands them over as `menu`, and the rail draws a section **Verktyg** with Kalender first and the tools in alphabetical order, four shown and the rest under "Fler verktyg" (`apps/web/src/components/nav.tsx`, `shell.tsx`). A row links to the tool's own page (D42, the row below). | Built 2026-11-05, first as one row per connector with a `menu_enabled` column (migration `0012`), changed the same day to per view (migration `0013_menu_per_view` carries the old choice over and drops the column). `connectors/telefonbok` names "Telefonbok" and "Mitt team". Not built: a row pinned by a person or by a manager for a unit, an audience for a row narrower than the connector's, the same choice for the chips under the box. |
| Tool pages | **A tool opens as a page (D42).** Everything under Verktyg is a page in the one shell: Kalender at its own address, a view chosen as a tool at `/tools/:capability` (`apps/web/src/pages/tool.tsx`, after Astryx's `table-page` template: the row's label as the `h1`, then the view through `DeclaredView` with `headingLevel={2}`). The view's controls rewrite `input` in the address with `replace`. A row's own view (a contact card) opens in `PanelView` over the page, addressed by `open`, `open-title`, `open-input` and `via` (`toolHref` in `components/nav.tsx`). The page exists only for a capability in the person's menu; anything else is "Verktyget finns inte". A table is a table from 1280 px (`tableQuery`, D48), a list below. A view opened from a chip, an answer, a search hit or a card still opens in the panel. | Built 2026-11-05. Not built: a link from a view in the panel to the tool's page, sorting and column filters, a canvas artboard for the page, a manual screen-reader pass. |
| Addresses | **Addresses are English (D43).** Every path, query parameter, parameter value and anchor the browser shows: `/overview` (`#proposals`, `#notices`, `?card=`), `/conversations/:id`, `/areas/:area`, `/waiting`, `/snoozed`, `/handled`, `/views/:capability?title=&input=&area=`, `/views/card/:threadId/:actionId`, `/calendar?date=&view=day\|week\|month`, `/tools/:capability`, `/admin/connectors`, `/admin/models`, `/admin/models/providers/:id`. The piles are `'waiting' \| 'snoozed' \| 'handled'` in the code, the calendar's view `'day' \| 'week' \| 'month'`. No redirects from the Swedish addresses: nothing is deployed and no stored row holds one. The old-to-new table is in the decision; the session log below and the decisions before D42 still name the old ones. | Built 2026-11-05. Not done: an area's id (`/areas/kalender`) is data from the connector and the threads table, and still Swedish; the stream's group names, the page mode, the admin tab ids and the answer shapes are Swedish identifiers that are not addresses. |
| Connector check | **Two results (D44):** `findings` is the English log for the CLI and never reaches the browser; `items` is what an administrator reads, Swedish, one row per fact, grouped under a verdict, with no answer from the service. Drawn by `apps/web/src/pages/admin/check-result.tsx`. |
| Structure | **Every surface has one job (D50).** The rail holds places and conversations (Nytt samtal, Att göra, Verktyg, Samtal); the column is a page or a conversation; the panel holds the view, source or card beside it and never the conversation. **Att göra** (`/todo?tab=needs-you\|waiting\|snoozed\|done&area=&card=`) is the one page for everything pushed: a card's state as tabs (a selector on a phone), the areas as a filter, the chosen row's card in the panel. **The panel** (`usePanel`, `PanelBody` in `components/panel.tsx`) is a `LayoutPanel` in the page's `end` slot where it fits beside the column (from 1296 px beside a transcript, 1336 px on Att göra, 1756 px beside a tool's table) and a drawer where it does not; what it holds is in the address (`panel-address.ts`: `open`, `open-title`, `open-input`, `via`, `open-card`, `open-action`, `card`). **The box is on every page**: on Att göra and a tool's page it sits at the foot, one line tall, its placeholder names what the page shows, and sending opens `/conversations/new` at once with that beside the transcript (where both fit) and as a chip; `ConversationProvider` sits above the pages in `main.tsx`. A question where no transcript is on the page starts a new conversation. | Accepted and built 2026-11-05. Replaces `/overview`, `/areas/:area`, `/waiting`, `/snoozed`, `/handled` and `/views/…` without redirects. Not built: the panel's tabs for what a conversation has opened, the box on the calendar page, a conversation's origin shown on its card or tool page, a resizable panel, a place for Läget and the day (they were on `/overview`), a manual screen-reader pass. |
| Answers while they are written | **The turn streams over server-sent events (D36):** `POST /api/conversation/stream` sends the reads as steps and the model's text as deltas, then the written turn; the question is drawn at once, the answer pending under it (skeleton, `ChatToolCalls`, `useStreamingText`), the box keeps focus with Stoppa; a stopped turn writes nothing. The landing's box never moves while the person types, and rests on the column's centre line when the brief fits in the upper half (D39, `docs/decisions/D39-the-landings-box-rests-on-the-columns-centre-line.md`); the model menu shows whenever a model answers; "Lägg till i samtalet" is an `@` icon button. Decided 2026-11-05. | `docs/decisions/D36-an-answer-is-drawn-as-it-is-written.md`. |
| Licensing | **Open source only.** Clew will be shared with other municipalities and governments. No closed-source dependencies, packages, images, fonts or required hosted services. | Decided 2026-11-03. Rule and checklist in `CLAUDE.md`. Licence audit of everything pinned is in section 3. |
| Theme and logo | **Saved in Clew's database on Administration › Utseende behind `admin.appearance`, read by every browser from the public `GET /api/appearance`; a theme is a Design Tokens (`.tokens.json`) file of Astryx's semantic colour tokens for light and dark, checked for WCAG contrast by the page and by the server; the logo is an uploaded PNG or SVG; Astryx Neutral with no logo until an administrator chooses (D46).** Accepted and built 2026-11-10, D98, replacing the build variables of D37/D38. | `packages/contracts/src/theme-file.ts` owns the format, the closed token list, the contrast pairs and the published schema (`docs/protocol/theme-file.schema.json`); `apps/server/src/modules/appearance/` the storage, permission and audit; `packages/ui/src/{branding,theme}.ts` the mapping to Astryx tokens over Neutral; `apps/web/src/branding.ts` the read at start; `themes/sundsvall/theme.tokens.json` Sundsvall's palette as an example file. A theme is colour only. Operators: `docs/maintainers/branding.md`. |
| Compact header, profile and reminders | **Compact navigation heading with the reminder bell beside the brand; account icon and full session name as the rail's last row, built like Områden; it expands to Inställningar (identity/unit, persisted light/dark appearance) and the existing logout. Bell reuses active thread reminders and hangs them from itself as a Popover, a sheet on a touch phone; separate read/unread notifications remain open.** Accepted 2026-11-05, D39. | `components/shell.tsx`, `components/reminders.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/appearance.tsx`, `theme.ts`, `apps/web/src/stream.ts`, `pages/start.tsx`, `tests/a11y/{shell,branding}.spec.ts`. |
| Administration in the rail | **The administration pages draw the same rail as every other page; the row Administration opens and closes like Områden, with Anslutna system and Modeller as links under it.** Accepted 2026-11-05, D45. | `components/nav.tsx`, `pages/admin/shell.tsx`, `tests/a11y/{admin,shell}.spec.ts`. |
| Density of a surface | **One visible next step, filled only when due today; the rest behind one Fler val; type base 15; area as a word, not a colour; 44 px under a finger through the theme; Kort, Nytt samtal and Funktioner as the words.** Accepted 2026-11-05. See `docs/decisions/D48-one-next-step-the-rest-one-step-away.md`. |

## 3. Backend: decided, kept for the record

Observability, 2026-11-09 (D91): licences checked with `npm view <package>@<version> license` before pinning exactly in `apps/server`: `pino` 10.4.0 MIT (its dependencies `sonic-boom`, `thread-stream`, `pino-std-serializers`, `pino-abstract-transport`, `@pinojs/redact`, `quick-format-unescaped`, `on-exit-leak-free`, `process-warning`, `atomic-sleep`, `real-require` and `safe-stable-stringify` MIT); `@opentelemetry/api` 1.10.0, `sdk-node` 0.301.0, `sdk-metrics` 3.1.0, `resources` 3.1.0, `exporter-trace-otlp-http` 0.301.0, `exporter-metrics-otlp-http` 0.301.0, `instrumentation-undici` 0.35.0 and `semantic-conventions` 1.45.0 Apache-2.0 (their dependencies `import-in-the-middle` Apache-2.0, `require-in-the-middle`, `module-details-from-path`, `cjs-module-lexer` and `es-module-lexer` MIT; `sdk-node` also brings the gRPC, proto, Prometheus and Zipkin exporters, all Apache-2.0, unused); `@hono/otel` 1.3.0 MIT; `@ai-sdk/otel` 1.0.166 Apache-2.0, which required `ai` 7.0.166 (Apache-2.0, from 7.0.162) so one copy is shared; `pino-pretty` 13.2.0 MIT as a development dependency. `@opentelemetry/exporter-logs-otlp-http` was added and removed the same session (logs go to stdout). The development stack gains `grafana/otel-lgtm:0.40.0` (AGPL-3.0, a separate process, development only, pinned by digest). Not taken: `@traceloop/node-server-sdk` and `@arizeai/openinference-vercel` (Apache-2.0, a second vocabulary over the AI SDK's own), `@opentelemetry/instrumentation-pg` (Clew uses `postgres.js`), Arize Phoenix (Elastic License 2.0), Sentry (Functional Source License). Nothing phones home: export is off until `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` is set.

Durable engine, 2026-11-09 (D85): one dependency replaced. `npm view @dbos-inc/dbos-sdk@5.4.0 license` is MIT, pinned exactly in `apps/server`; its dependencies `pg`, `commander`, `superjson` and `serialize-error` are MIT and `yaml` ISC. The four `@temporalio/*` 1.25.0 packages and the `temporalio/auto-setup` and `temporalio/ui` images leave the repository and the devcontainer. The engine runs in the BFF process against Clew's own PostgreSQL; no new image, service, account or telemetry.

MCP development integration, 2026-11-08 (D81): checked `npm view @modelcontextprotocol/sdk version license` before pinning `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk@1.32.2` in the server; MIT. The independent mocks reuse the existing Node image (`node:26.13.0-bookworm-slim`); no new image family. Provider UI remains a vendor artifact, using the D81 iframe exception; the generic Clew surface uses Astryx.

Document catalogue, 2026-11-08 (D79): licence checked with npm before exact pins in `packages/ui`: `pdfjs-dist` 6.5.438 Apache-2.0, `docx-preview` 0.4.1 Apache-2.0, `read-excel-file` 9.3.10 MIT. These run locally with no vendor service, licence key or telemetry. Renderers are lazy-loaded; PDF and workbook parsing use workers. No Eneo dependencies or source files were changed.

Microsoft 365 in the development stack, 2026-11-06 (D69, second amendment): no package or image dependencies added or changed. The `graph` service runs on the existing `clew-dev:local` image; the new scripts and tests use what is already pinned.

Reliability continuation, 2026-11-05: no package or image dependencies added or changed. The new CI lane uses the existing pinned PostgreSQL/Temporal/Node stack and existing libraries; no additional licence review was needed.

Hono on Node was chosen (see section 2). The comparison that led there, with npm weekly downloads on 2026-11-03:

| Option | Downloads | Fit for Clew |
|---|---|---|
| **Hono + `@hono/node-server`** | 101M / 95M | Web-standard `Request`/`Response`; AI SDK and MCP SDK plug in without adapters; same code runs on Bun later. Chosen. |
| Fastify 5 + TypeBox provider | 21.7M / 0.9M | Most proven Node server; needs the Node adapters for AI SDK and MCP. The conservative alternative. |
| Express 5 / NestJS / Elysia / Koa | | Not chosen: no typing story, too much structure, Bun-only, or declining. |

Licence audit 2026-11-03, all open source: Astryx (`@astryxdesign/*`, github.com/meta/astryx) MIT; `@stylexjs/stylex`, `hono`, `@hono/node-server`, `drizzle-kit`, `openid-client`, `jose`, `fast-xml-parser`, `temporal-polyfill`, `@sinclair/typebox`, `ajv`, `ajv-formats`, `tsx`, `react`, `react-dom`, `react-router`, `vite`, `@vitejs/plugin-react` MIT; `drizzle-orm`, `ai`, `@ai-sdk/openai-compatible`, `typescript` Apache-2.0; `postgres` Unlicense; `ical.js` MPL-2.0; `lucide-react` ISC; `intl-messageformat` BSD-3-Clause. Infrastructure: Keycloak Apache-2.0, Nextcloud and RoomVox AGPL-3.0 (separate containers, synthetic dev calendar only), Mailpit MIT, PostgreSQL licence, Node MIT, Bun MIT, Temporal server and UI MIT. Added 2026-11-04: `@temporalio/activity`, `@temporalio/client`, `@temporalio/worker`, `@temporalio/workflow` 1.25.0, all MIT (checked with `npm view`). Added 2026-11-04 (session 13): `dependency-cruiser` 17.4.3 MIT; `ajv`, `ajv-formats` and `jose` now also at the root for scripts and tests (already audited). `connectors/hello` has no dependencies; its image is `node:26.13.0-bookworm-slim` (MIT). Added 2026-11-04 (session 15): `fast-check` 4.10.2 MIT (checked with `npm view`), development only, property tests of domain functions. Added 2026-11-04 (session 19, D27): `@fullcalendar/react` 7.1.4 MIT, with `@fullcalendar/core` 7.1.4 MIT and `@full-ui/headless-calendar` 7.1.4 MIT as its dependencies (checked with `npm view`); `temporal-polyfill` 1.0.7 declared by `packages/ui` as its peer. No Premium package. ## 4. Open questions

Notification follow-up (D39, 2026-11-05): existing reminders and important links first; separate read/unread semantics, retention and other notification kinds remain open.

Product questions from the design review, still unanswered:

1. Is the 2026 KPI surface a hard commitment that must be visible at a glance, or exception-based plus on demand? The built "Läget" row assumes "at a glance".
2. Is Göra/Hitta/Veta/Förstå a UI requirement from the taktisk grupp, or internal framing? Clew hides it.
3. Is the read receipt a compliance record, and must "Viktigt nu" always be visible? Clew gives it a header counter ("1 av 3 avbrott i dag").
4. Is there appetite for standing consent on reversible actions? Clew v1 says no.
5. Who owns news and documents once the new intranet exists?
6. Explored on the canvas 2026-11-03, not decided: first login as a welcome thread (no wizard), a time-boxed "Min första vecka" area for new employees (UC-OMAR-1), and notifications as deliveries of thread state over three channels (page, push for same-day deadlines only, e-mail digest at bundle times) under the attention budget. User-created reminders and watches are the user's own triggers in the same model; watches with conditions are what brings in Temporal. Läget moved from a strip into the sidebar, one line per area. "Väntar inte på dig" demoted from a section to one collapsed line at the end of the stream. Completion drawn as a countdown to zero plus a done state that names the work (row "Att bli klar"). Hassan's shift drawn as three phone frames: before, during (voice incident report with field-by-field confirmation), after (row "En dag med Hassan"). Answers have three shapes, Svar, Vy and Förslag, and a view is an addressable deterministic page the composer can ask about (row "Svar och vyer"; `docs/architecture/integrations.md`, Answers and views). Open: whether a per-person view counts as "inside the source system" for the names guardrail.

7. Errands (session 11, 2026-11-04): `docs/reviews/2026-11-04-support-management-integration-review.md` proposes D18, errands as a Clew-owned pass-through domain (`ErrandProvider` in `packages/errands`, SupportManagement as the first adapter, CaseData second). Open before building: which test namespace and WSO2 credential Clew may use; whether the `clew` realm's `preferred_username` is the AD account the `X-Sent-By` header needs; whether a Clew-rendered errand view may show stakeholder names (question 6); whether to open the webhook-channel issue on `api-service-support-management` now. Since session 30 (D35) the hosting is one connector per case system with a shared errand profile in the protocol; the order of work is the profile in `protocol.ts` and the schema, a connector `propose` whose `ThreadDraft` becomes a card on demand, buttons on the panel's rows ("Öppna som kort"), `clew.errands`, then the `sm` connector against a test namespace.

General Q&A over documents (D26, built in session 25 on branch `worktree-fraga-d26`, to be merged into `main`, a fast-forward from `5a92b2f`): a person asks "what do I do when X" and the answer lives in a routine. What exists: the `SearchHit` contract and `kinds` on capabilities; the core connector `clew` (`apps/server/src/modules/search/`) with the composite `clew.search` (every `<id>.search` in scope at once, 3 s per source inside 4 s, stale marked in code, missed sources named), `clew.openSource`, `clew.fetchSource` and the propose `clew.cardFromAnswer`; the router's catch-all without a model; the model path's document rules, `[n]` citations stored as `sources` in the message meta, "Steg" as an answer shape, follow-ups from the model's trailer line with the document's headings as fallback; the `document` chip resolved by refetching; the sources view in the panel with "Öppna" and "Lägg till i samtalet"; the threads use case that makes a card of a cited answer; delegation (`delegation` in a manifest, `X-Clew-Delegated-Token`, `auth-exchange.ts`, `context.delegate`, Keycloak client `eneo` and scope `eneo-audience` from `auth-setup.ts`, `scripts/auth-exchange-check.ts` green against the dev Keycloak); and `connectors/eneo` against recorded Eneo answers. Decide whether the audience is per connector only (proposed) or also per area. The admin screens need a canvas row before they are built.

Still open: the FullCalendar time grid is not a WAI-ARIA grid (the panel list and the dialogs stay the accessible path); the live Chrome pass against Nextcloud (create, move, cancel, drag); a manual screen-reader pass; the canvas has no artboard for the month view, the phone list or the two dialogs; an Astryx `Calendar` cannot mark days that hold events (gap recorded in D32, not filed).
