Reliability implementation checkpoint, 2026-11-05: implemented D44–D46 from the approved architecture plan. Authorization defaults to deny, source-derived reads share current-access checks, protocol 2 uses installation RS256 authority with separate callback grants and public-key rotation, and generic/nested invocation cannot execute writes. Generic card decisions now persist encrypted execution input/result, approval audit, dispatch outbox and card projection atomically; Temporal dispatches the immutable intent, ambiguous outcomes read back under the same key, and compensation keeps the original receipt until confirmed. The independent reference source uses durable SQLite and fault injection. Authenticated SSE carries committed revisions, clears retained browser state on authorization changes, and reconnects after transient failures. Revision triggers are deferred to avoid business/counter lock inversion. Open work is no longer displaced by the 50-entry history limit. Migrations 0014–0019 are applied locally. The login screenshot exposed a regression in the new readiness gate: failed initialization stayed blocked and the guard covered login. Startup now retries every five seconds and authentication routes are independent; live readiness returned 200, login 303, unauthenticated threads 401 and public verification keys 200.

Validation at this checkpoint: `bun run check` passes (333 unit/web tests, four Node tests, type/dependency/purity checks, generated docs and the signed connector walkthrough); `bun run test:db` passes 30 cases, including concurrent change-revision writers; `bun run test:durability` passes against actual PostgreSQL, Temporal and an independent Node source with a lost response, concurrent approval, source restart and outbox redelivery. Browser results are recorded in the implementation verification report. No manual screen-reader pass or real-source pilot has been completed. The Playwright wrapper cannot run inside this devcontainer because it has no Docker CLI; the same configuration is run directly with its pinned Chromium installed inside the container. No new proprietary dependency: Ajv and ajv-formats use the existing pinned MIT packages; SQLite is part of the Node runtime. Calendar write migration, occurrence/refresh lifecycle, transactional timer cancellation, attention admission and broader operational proof remain pending; see the checkpoint in the plan. The real-system pilot source remains undecided. Parallel integrator documentation is included without renumbering historical decisions.

Integrator documentation, 2026-11-05: reviewed the documentation and all 47 decision files against the current connector implementation, preserving the existing authorization/execution work in this checkout. README and docs now lead with the platform overview and connector authoring path. Added focused authorization, actions/recovery, testing/deployment and current-support guides; moved full-stack setup and installation branding into maintainer pages. Older architecture designs have explicit reading-status notes; the old protocol path links to the current reference. Decision bodies and filenames are preserved, including the D37–D39 collisions; generated indexes include D44 and all reviews. `llms.txt` and generated `docs/context.json` provide the coding-agent entry point and source map. `scripts/docs.ts` generates the schema, real example manifests, field reference and indexes; `scripts/docs-smoke.ts` exercises the real phone book handler with ephemeral RS256 authority, required-input lookup, actor binding and credential rejection. Added `docs:generate`, `docs:smoke`, `docs:check`, a docs-only CI workflow and documentation validation in `bun run check`. Corrected stale protocol-signing comments and connector setup examples, without changing runtime behavior. Validation: full `bun run check` passed in the devcontainer (types, dependency rules, purity, 332 unit/web tests, four Node runtime tests, generated references, local links and signed fixture walkthrough). Deliberate temporary drift, missing-file links and missing-heading links were all rejected and restored. No browser, live-source or durability-service suite was required or run for this documentation work. Review: `docs/reviews/2026-11-05-integrator-documentation-review.md`.

Architecture follow-up plan, 2026-11-05: the product owner asked for a plan addressing the most consequential review findings and explicitly chose to leave the first real-system pilot source open. Saved `docs/architecture/reliability-and-authorization-plan.md`: six phases covering authorization/connector trust, one durable approved action, migration of every existing source-write path (including calendar, without choosing it as the pilot), occurrence identity and isolated refresh, reliable timer/delivery/attention behavior, and real-stack plus selected-source readiness. The plan names reviewable increments, implementation seams, failure-based exit criteria, migration/rollback rules and decisions still to record. It recommends PostgreSQL execution/timer intent with a transactional outbox into Temporal, source-specific idempotency/read-back, and authenticated SSE invalidation while preserving D33's no-browser-polling policy. D37–D39 are not renumbered; all new architecture choices remain proposed. Documentation links and whitespace checked; no application code, dependencies, configuration or external systems changed and no application tests rerun for this documentation-only work.

Architecture review, 2026-11-05, against revision `4696f6d`: reviewed the vision, personas, decisions and architecture against the implementation. Saved `docs/reviews/2026-11-05-architecture-against-vision.md` and linked it from `docs/README.md`. Recommendation: keep the modular monolith, PostgreSQL, connector services, deterministic use cases, optional model and Temporal; make the next milestone one reliable real-source vertical slice. Synthetic probes confirmed connector-minted identity assertions, concurrent duplicate execution, a source/local commit gap, assistant access to disabled-connector cards, dismissed occurrences returning, stale cards after source-side resolution, whole-scan failure, uncertain undo losing its receipt and unchecked capability output. Other findings cover startup governance, timer/DB coordination, background delivery, attention admission, identity/data/hosting decisions and documentation drift. `bun run check` passed (307 tests, four Node runtime tests, types, dependencies and purity); the PostgreSQL thread-store contract passed six tests. Probes used only synthetic actors, memory stores and fake transports. No application code, dependencies, decisions or connector configuration changed; recommendations are not accepted roadmap decisions.

Session 42, 2026-11-05 (branch `feature/astryx-neutral-standardtema` from `main` at `c995d68`, built in the tree of `feature/notiser-och-kontonamn` while another session changed the reminders there, and moved here as one commit): the product owner wanted the rail to stop being as white as the page. The Sundsvall token fix was taken back out and is described in D46 under Switching back; the Sundsvall profile is otherwise unchanged. The default header now has no logo, as D38 allows; `VITE_HEADER_LOGO_SRC` and `VITE_HEADER_LOGO_ALT` bring one back on Neutral. `tests/a11y/branding.spec.ts` has a new test that the rail differs from the page and the selected row from what is behind it, in both appearances and the phone drawer, skipped on Sundsvall until D46's two changes are made. The full suite now runs on Neutral and CI's second step on `sundsvall` and `custom`. Verified: `bun run check` exits 0; the full WCAG suite on Neutral gave 227 passed, 34 skipped, and one failure that was the other session's temporary `zz-admin-shot.spec.ts` disappearing during the run; `branding.spec.ts` passes on all three profiles (the new test skipped on Sundsvall). Screenshots checked for Neutral in light and dark mode. No manual screen reader pass: colours only, no new flow.

Session 41, 2026-11-05 (pushed directly to `main` at the product owner's request, on top of `15c7a7f`; the branch `feature/notiser-och-kontonamn` does not have this commit): the product owner found it messy that Administration replaced the whole menu. D45: `AdminShell` now uses `useRail` like every other page, its own rail is gone, and Administration opens and closes like Områden, with Anslutna system and Modeller as links under it, collapsed until one of them is open. The text Om administrationen repeated the lead on Anslutna system and went with the row Till startsidan; the heading and the breadcrumbs lead home. Two other shapes were built and dropped: `href` with `collapsible`, because Astryx draws that toggle's chevron 2 px left of the other rows' (223 against 225 px), and a plain link with `collapsible={false}`, which the product owner rejected because the row could not be opened and closed. `tests/a11y/admin.spec.ts` walks from the start page through both sections and closes the row again by keyboard, and `shell.spec.ts` holds its chevron under Områden's. Verified in the shared checkout, which also held other sessions' unfinished reminder, theme and default-profile edits: the `admin`, `models`, `shell` and `menu` specs pass (68 tests), and typecheck, dependency rules and purity pass; the red unit tests seen along the way came from those other edits. Each suite run empties the shared `test-results`, so parallel runs fail with `ENOENT`; `--output=test-results/<name>` avoids that. Seen in screenshots at 1280 and 320 px with mock data, not in a logged-in browser; no screen reader pass, and not compared with the design canvas. Afterwards, CI's only failure was `tests/a11y/answer.spec.ts:11`, a race in the test and not a product fault: the mock answer ran on a 1.5 s timer while the axe scan takes about 1.1 s here and longer on a runner, so the answer could arrive first and Enter then rightly sent the next question. Slowing the CPU four times reproduced CI's exact failure. The test now holds the answer with `api.holdAnswer()` and lets go of it when it is done; use that, not `answerDelay`, for a test that works while an answer is pending. `tests/a11y/branding.spec.ts:79` (D46) failed 3 runs in 6 for a similar reason (`openNav` inside a poll, straight after a resize) and now opens the drawer once, 15 of 15. Both fixes were pushed directly to `main` at the product owner's request.

Session 33, continued, 2026-11-05 (after session 34; worktree `meny-verktyg`, branch `meny-verktyg` from `main` at `e50ef22`, rebased onto `a32f9a9` when session 34's D39 landed while this was being built, so this decision is D40; brought into `main` at the product owner's request; the main tree still held an earlier session's uncommitted D37 proposal, left as it was): the product owner ran the phone book live, asked how it stays put in the UI, and proposed the menu, in a section with the calendar. **D40** written and built. The manifest may carry `menu: { capability, label, icon }` (`MenuDeclarationSchema` in `packages/contracts`, `$defs.Menu` in the published schema, `Connector.menu` for a bundled one): one row per connector, on a read of its own that declares a view and needs no input (`menuProblem`), with an icon named from Clew's list of eight. The row is an offer: `connector.menu_enabled` is false by default (migration `0012_connector_menu`, **run `bun run db:init` after pulling**; it is applied to the dev database), and "Visa i menyn" on the connector's page (Områden) or in the wizard switches it on through the admin use case `update`, one audit row `connector.menu`, live at once. `Governance.menuEnabled` is optional and absent means off, so the existing `govern` calls did not change. `Registry.menu(actor)` filters by audience, state, the switch and the read's own scope and sorts by label; `GET /api/areas` answers `{ areas, menu }`; the rail (`useRail`, `Shell`'s new `tools` slot) draws **Verktyg** above the hidden "Alltid" section with Kalender, then the tools, four shown and the rest under a collapsible "Fler verktyg"; `RailCurrent.view` marks the open tool; the menu's search finds a tool. The admin detail carries `menu` and `menuEnabled`, the preview the row's label, and the manifest diff says `menu: added | removed | changed` (the product owner's stored telefonbok manifest predates the row, so "Hämta manifest igen" shows "Ny rad för menyn"). `connectors/telefonbok` offers "Telefonbok" on its search; its README now has the devcontainer way to run it. Verified: `bun run check` (280 unit tests; new `tests/unit/menu.test.ts`, 10, asserting the audit row), `bun run test:db` (23, `menuEnabled` in the store contract on Postgres), `tests/a11y/menu.spec.ts` (10 new: the named section and its order, Enter on the row, `aria-current`, focus back on the row after Esc, the menu's search, more than four tools, the phone drawer, 320 px, the administrator's switch and its disabled state, axe), the whole WCAG suite, and a screenshot of the rail (`screens.views.spec.ts`, "verktyg in the menu"). Learned while testing: a panel opened fresh takes focus on the dialog itself and announces nothing through `useAnnounce` (the dialog's name is what is heard), and `reflowProblems` cannot be used with the phone drawer open, because the drawer's own truncated Pågår rows and the visually hidden "Alltid" heading read as clipped. Not done: a view as a full page (from the calendar the row leads to the start page with the panel open), a row pinned by a person or a manager, a canvas artboard for Verktyg (none exists), a screen-reader pass. **Changed the same afternoon to a choice per view**, after the product owner had switched the row on live and said one switch per connector was too narrow: a connector exposes several views, and which are tools must be configurable. D40 was rewritten in place (its file is now `D40-views-can-be-tools-in-the-menu-and-an-administrator-chooses-which.md`): every read with a declared view that opens without input can be a tool; `menu` moved from the top of the manifest to the capability and is only the row's name and icon (`MenuHintSchema`); the choice is `menu: true` in the capability's override, set through `update` with `menu: { name: boolean }` and one audit row `connector.menu` per view; `Governance.menuEnabled`, `ConnectorSummary.menuEnabled`, `ConnectorDetail.menu` and `ManifestDiff.menu` are gone, and `ConnectorCapabilityView.menu` (`{ label, icon, on } | null`) and `ConnectorPreview.menu` (labels) took their place. The page's single switch became the list "Verktyg i menyn" under Områden, the same on a phone; the wizard no longer asks. Migration `0013_menu_per_view` moves a switched-on row to the view it named, strips the top-level `menu` from stored manifests (the loader would refuse them at boot) and drops `menu_enabled`; it was run on a scratch database with rows in the old shape before it touched the dev database. `connectors/telefonbok` names "Telefonbok" and "Mitt team". Verified: `bun run check` (285 unit tests; `menu.test.ts` rewritten, 15), `bun run test:db` (23), the WCAG suite (188 passed, 30 skipped; `menu.spec.ts` now 11). While this was built, `main` was rebased onto `origin/main` by a pull that brought the municipal profiles and the compact header: the decisions folder now holds two files numbered D37, two numbered D38 and two numbered D39 (the pulled ones and the local ones), which nobody has sorted out yet.

Session 33, 2026-11-05 (worktree `telefonbok-vyer`, branch `telefonbok-vyer` from `main` at `d4c2ace`, brought into `main` at the product owner's request; another session had a proposed decision uncommitted in the main tree under the number D37, so this one is D38): the product owner brought a test case, the organisation's own phone book as a connector, and asked how a connector attaches a view that works without a model and is not an iframe with the connector's own design. Research first (review `docs/reviews/2026-11-05-phone-book-and-declared-views.md`: the gaps in the search contract, the names rule, the guessed panel view; MCP Apps read and ruled out because its only content type is HTML in a sandboxed iframe; A2UI weighed; the start-page prototype at start.sundsvall.dev read for the views it needs), then built. **D38**: a read capability may declare a `view` (`ViewSchema` in `packages/contracts`: `table`, `list`, `detail`, `text`; fields with label and format; `input.query` and `input.filter` bound to the capability's input; `open` to a read of the same connector; `modelReads`). The rules are `viewProblem` in `packages/connectors/src/views.ts`, enforced by `parseManifest` and `Registry.register`, and `checkConnector` exercises a view against a real answer. The server hands the declaration to the browser (`registry.viewFor`, `GET /api/cap/:name` answers `{ output, view }`, a card's read action too). The browser has one renderer, `apps/web/src/components/view.tsx`, used by `PanelView` (a declared view first, the generic rules as the fallback; a table gets a wide panel and is a list below 900 px) and by the compact embed under the latest answer (`ViewEmbed` in `conversation.tsx`, only for a `ViewRef` marked `declared`). The panel now stays open while another view loads (`shownView` in `start.tsx`), moves focus with the view and names it aloud, and `&via=1` gives a view opened from another its Tillbaka. The assistant: a tool whose view says `modelReads: 'summary'` runs but hands the model `viewSummary` (shown, how many rows), the answer carries the declared view, the router takes what was typed beyond the capability's name as the view's question (`viewInput`) and answers with a count (`viewAnswer`), `searchAnswer` counts people instead of naming them, and `citationsOf` leaves person hits out of what is stored. A composite search hit carries the view its source declares for a row (`SearchSourceRef.open`), and a hit that is not a document shows no review badge. **`connectors/telefonbok`**: reads only (`directory` as a table with a search field and a unit filter, `team` as a list, `person` as a detail, `search` and `fetch` as the people search source), the area Kontakter with the team list and the search as standing actions, the phone book behind a port in `directory.mjs` (an HTTP adapter whose record shape is an assumption, `toPerson` as the one mapping to adapt, a made-up organisation with a protected record that is never shown). Verified: `bun run check` (270 unit tests, among them `telefonbok-connector`, `views` and two conversation scenarios asserting the audit row and that no name or number is stored), `bun run test:db` (23), `scripts/connector-check.ts` against the connector over HTTP (all checks passed), and the WCAG suite from the worktree (`tests/a11y/views.spec.ts`, 10 new tests: roles, keyboard order, focus on a view change, the spoken count, reflow at 320 px, axe; 174 passed in the whole suite). One test in that suite, `keyboard.spec.ts` "Esc closes the conversation and focus stays in the row", fails about one run in four on untouched `main` as well; it is not from this session and is not fixed. Not done: a live pass in Chrome against the running stack (it needs a sign-in at Keycloak and the connector registered by an administrator), a screen-reader pass, and a canvas artboard for a declared view (none exists; `tests/a11y/screens.views.spec.ts` takes the screenshots a comparison will need). Open, in the review: whether the phone book is Metakatalogen, the real API's shape and whether it takes the IdP's tokens, a team list curated and pinned by a manager (a decision of its own), a person in several units, and a scope in the composite search. Later the same day the product owner started the connector in the devcontainer (`CLEW_CONNECTOR_SECRET=… node connectors/telefonbok/server.mjs`, registered at `http://127.0.0.1:4020`) and added it under Administration › Anslutna system, where the check failed on `telefonbok.person`, `.search` and `.fetch` ("Ogiltig referens", "Ogiltig sökning"). Cause: the admin page's `checkConnector` was composed without `acceptsEmptyInput`, whose default was to call every read with `{}`, so a read that needs input was called and its correct refusal counted as a fault; `scripts/connector-check.ts` passed a validator and never showed it, and `hello` has no such read. Fix: the default in `packages/connectors/src/check.ts` now leaves a read uncalled when its input schema lists `required` properties, and the composition root hands in the server's Ajv rule (`acceptsEmptyInput` in `apps/server/src/connectors/registry.ts`), the same as the script. `tests/unit/telefonbok-connector.test.ts` now runs the check without a validator, as the admin page did.

Session 32, 2026-11-05 (worktree `card-state`, branch `card-state-in-context` from `main` at `b42ece2`, one commit, fast-forwarded into `main` at the product owner's request; session 31 was editing `apps/server/src/assistant/` uncommitted in the main tree, so this was built aside and session 31's work was carried across uncommitted on top of the merge): the product owner approved a leave request in a conversation, then wrote "Eller vänta, jag måste avslå den förfrågan", and the model answered twice "Klicka på knappen Avslå på kortet", a button the card no longer showed: an acted-on card shows only Ångra allt and its read buttons while the undo window is open, and Avslå comes back only after Ångra allt. Cause: `serialiseContext` listed every `thread.actions` entry as "Knappar" whatever the card's state. Fix: `apps/server/src/assistant/context.ts` now writes the card's state and the buttons it shows right now, by the rules of `ActionRow` in `apps/web/src/components/card.tsx` (open: primary, two secondaries, Inte nu, Påminn mig, Fler val; snoozed: Ta upp nu; waiting: Påminn mig; handled with a receipt: the headline and the receipt rows with their outcome, then Ångra allt with its end time and the read buttons, and that the card's own buttons come back only after Ångra allt; after the window: nothing, change it in the source; dismissed: nothing), for a chipped card and for a chipped row's card. `serialiseContext` takes `now`; the system prompt gains the rule to name only buttons under "Knappar på kortet nu" and, for a handled card, to say so first and what can be done now. Two unit tests in `tests/unit/assistant.test.ts`. If `ActionRow` changes what a state shows, `cardState` must follow; a shared function in `@clew/contracts` would remove that pairing and was left for later because the other session had `card.tsx` and the contracts open.

Session 32, 2026-11-05 (branch `feature/kommunprofil-och-header`): installation branding and the compact profile/reminder navigation. Its validation URL is http://localhost:3020 and the window ends around 10:53:44 UTC (11:53:44 Stockholm). Health and fresh login-form reachability were checked; no credential or business-data reset. Session 31, 2026-11-05 (in the dev tree on top of the uncommitted D34 work of sessions 26 to 28, nothing committed; the admin session had committed and stopped at 10:20): the product owner reported that the landing's box jumped while they typed and that sending showed nothing until the whole answer was back; during the session they also asked for the model selector in the box, for "Lägg till i samtalet" as an icon, and for the brief rows' actions to be centred vertically. D36 written and built. The server streams a turn: `POST /api/conversation/stream` (server-sent events through Hono's `streamSSE`) sends each capability the turn reads as a `step` and the model's text as `text` deltas, then `done` with the `ConversationResponse`. `converse` takes `{ emit, signal }` and still writes the turn and its audit row in one transaction at the end. A closed connection throws `TurnStopped` before the write, and the model is not marked down. `createGenerator` runs on `streamText` and reports each step's text through `onText`, and `visibleSoFar` holds back the "Följdfrågor:" trailer. The browser draws a `PendingTurn` at once (the question, `Skeleton` lines, `ChatToolCalls` for the reads, `useStreamingText` into `Markdown`). Rows are keyed by position, so the written turn replaces the pending one in place and the log speaks the answer once. While an answer is pending the box keeps focus and stays open with Stoppa in place of send (`isStopShown`/`onStop`); Enter is held back through `ChatComposerInput`'s `onKeyDown` seam. Astryx's disabled "interruptible" composer was tried first and dropped: it dims the whole composer to 60 %, and axe failed the scope line. Stopped or failed, the question goes back in the box. The landing's brief and box are one top-anchored block (the old `vAlign="between"` moved the box by half of every change in "Menar du"). `BriefSkeleton` stands in for the brief and the overview's Nu until `/api/threads` answers. `ModelMenu` shows whenever a model answers. `components/add-to-conversation.tsx` is the one "Lägg till i samtalet" button: a ghost `IconButton` with Heroicons' `AtSymbolIcon` (new dependency `@heroicons/react` 2.2.0, MIT, section 3), the words as name and tooltip, "I samtalet" with a check once added. The brief rows use `ListItem align="center"` on desktop. Tests: unit scenarios for the events, a stop that writes nothing (also after the answer is known), the reset when the model fails halfway, the trailer held back, and the streamed SDK wiring and its abort; WCAG `tests/a11y/answer.spec.ts` (the pending turn with axe, typing ahead, Stoppa, the box not moving under "Menar du", the brief's skeleton); the one-model menu and the icon's names updated in `models`, `structure` and `keyboard`. `bun run check` green (249 unit tests), the WCAG suite 165 passed and 19 skipped. Verified in Chrome as Hillevi against gemma4-31b-it: the box stays put while typing; the question appears at once with skeleton lines and Stoppa; the Heroma read shows as a step; the text streams; the written turn lands on `/samtal/:id`; Stoppa on a follow-up put it back in the box, and the database holds two messages and one audit row for that conversation. Not done: H1's after-the-fact line of what the assistant read (steps are live only), linked citations while streaming, a skeleton when opening an earlier conversation from the rail, a manual screen-reader pass of the pending state, and the dev tree's `bun run dev` was not restarted (it picked up the new dependency and route on its own). Later the same session, after the commit, the product owner asked how a connector that depends on another is handled and whether connectors need versions; D37 is written as proposed (no code): `uses` in the manifest, a capability's name as its contract with deprecation, compatibility verdicts per dependent, an audit row for the manifest refetched at boot (today it goes live silently), and B's output validated on cross-connector calls.

Session 30, 2026-11-05 (worktree `arenden-d35`, branch `arenden-d35` from `main` at `a2838e2`, one commit, fast-forwarded into `main` at the product owner's request; another session was committing to `main` and the D34 work of sessions 26 to 28 was still uncommitted in the main tree, so this was built aside and that work was carried across uncommitted on top of the merge): the product owner asked how no-model mode shows errands, how the connector API is read, and how a person would act on an errand from three errand systems. D35 written and accepted: one connector per case system, an errand profile of reserved reads in the protocol (`<id>.errands`, `<id>.errand`, `<id>.errandCard`, `<id>.search` with `kinds: ['case']`), each system's executes its own, a core `clew.errands` that combines the lists, and a card is one system's; D18's hosting shape replaced, its other rules kept, its status updated. Built from D35, the card-action rule only: `Registry.draftProblem` and `RegistryOptions.onRefused` (`packages/connectors/src/index.ts`); `Registry.scan` refuses a draft whose action names another connector's capability or the wrong kind, the server logs it (`apps/server/src/connectors/registry.ts`), `connector-check` fails on it (`packages/connectors/src/check.ts`); `act` (`apps/server/src/threads/usecases/cards.ts`) takes the kind from the registry, refuses a foreign button in Swedish, and drops an `undoCapability` that is not an execute of the same connector, recording `undoRefused` on the `thread.acted` audit row. The gap was found while answering: `scan` passed drafts through and `act` trusted the draft's `kind`, so a card could carry another connector's execute or run an execute as a read without receipt, audit row or undo. Docs: `connector-protocol.md` (the rule after the manifest rules), `creating-a-connector.md` (the scan paragraph). Verified: `bun run check` (237 unit, 7 new: two in `connectors.test.ts`, four in `threads.test.ts` asserting the audit rows, one in `remote-connector.test.ts` running `checkConnector` against a tampered `hello`; deps and purity clean). Compared the live page with G, the G phone and H1 to H4 and amended D34 (section "Amended 2026-11-05"). The shell has no TopNav any more: `Shell` renders the rail as the `shell-side-nav` template does (SideNavHeading with the person, Ny fråga, the search, the surface's sections, Kalender/Områden/Administration/Logga ut at the foot) and takes `nav` from `useRail()` in `components/nav.tsx`; AppShell `elevated` for the grey rail. The rail's search is a `Typeahead` over the cache (conversations, cards, areas); conversations group by Stockholm day; Områden is collapsed by default. The landing: the brief as a List in a Card with a fixed day column and "Granska" for cards that need a choice, the heading centred at display size, the box without a scope line, "Menar du" back under it (a matching card first), eight centred chips, the day as one line at the foot; on a phone the box is docked with the heading and chips above it. The box's scope line depends on where it sits (`Composer place`), ⌘K starts a new question from a conversation, the conversation toolbar says when it was started. The theme is plain Neutral again (the blue accent was never on the canvas). The Raindance mock says "en faktura" for one. WCAG suite: the box's accessible name is "Vad vill du göra"/"Svara i samtalet", structure tests check one navigation and the rail's heading, `openAreas()` opens the collapsed areas, the focus-ring helper waits for running CSS transitions (an Astryx field's ring animates in over 125 ms), two new tests (Menar du, the rail's search). `bun run check` green, the suite 136 passed and 11 skipped. Verified in Chrome at 1440 px and about 500 px wide: `/`, typing in the box, a conversation, `/oversikt`. Later the same morning the product owner asked where "Lägg till i samtalet" had gone (the brief rows had dropped it) and asked for `@` to stop listing everything: the brief rows carry it again (under the text on a phone), and `listObjects(persona, q, near)` scopes `@` to the box's context (D34, amended), with a unit test in `tests/unit/conversation.test.ts`, the mock API honouring `near`, and a WCAG test for the token and the scoped menu; the why line no longer ends in "..". Suite 137 passed, 11 skipped. Then "make the answer the card like H1": `MessageMeta.cards` (contracts), `matchCards` and `cardPath` in the assistant, the transcript's `TurnCard` drawing the page's card in the turn (live from the cache, once per conversation); scenario tests in `tests/unit/conversation.test.ts` (the card, the audit row's `cards`, not drawn twice, a chipped card drawn), the mock answering "attestera …" with a card, a structure test and an axe test. Suite 140 passed, 11 skipped; `bun run check` green. The live check ran as Mikael (signed in in that browser), who has no Raindance card, so it fell to the document search as it should; Hillevi's case is only verified through the mocked suite. Not done: the overview (artboard A) was only checked, not reworked (its footer box spans the full 1180 px); the items D34 lists as not built; a manual screen-reader pass.

Session 27, 2026-11-04 (late evening, in the dev tree, nothing committed; session 26's changes were still uncommitted and are built on): the product owner asked for a start page with real overview instead of one list, then chose "fråga först". Design exploration on the canvas (link in section 1): A the overview in three bands, B inbox with reading pane, C a board, D the phone, E the card's action ladder (the read-receipt card has "Jag har läst" but nothing to read: the kommunikation mock delivers no document), F what each persona is fed, G the box with the push as a brief, H1 the conversation, H2 a running job, H3 the rail expanded with the rules for notifications, H4 the page without the model. Corrections along the way: not everything is an action with a timeline (four kinds: requirement, proposal, event, figure), and the platform must stand without the model (D8). Built as D34: `cardKind` in contracts with unit tests; `apps/web/src/stream.ts` groups once for the page and the rail; `components/nav.tsx` is the rail (replaces `areas.tsx`, serves the calendar page too); `components/brief.tsx` and `PrimaryAction` in `card.tsx`; `pages/start.tsx` has two modes (`fraga` at `/` and `/samtal/:id`, `oversikt` at `/oversikt`, the areas, the piles and card views; `/vy/:capability` is the box unless an area is named); `theme.ts` is Neutral with a blue accent, tinted body and rail, `GridSpan` exported from `@clew/ui`; `figuresQuery` and `completionsQuery` added. The TopNav keeps identity, the attention line and Logga ut. The a11y suite was moved to the new routes (card tests on `/oversikt`, the piles as rail rows, a new test for the box and the brief) and the mock API serves `/figures` and `/completions`. `bun run check` green. Not built (D34 lists them): the rail's search field, the conversation's "something happened" dot, job steps on a running conversation, the `step` on the action contract. Verified in Chrome at 1440 px: `/`, `/oversikt`, a question opening `/samtal/:id`; the phone layout only through the suite. The composer's scope line reads "Det här samtalet" in a conversation.

Session 26, 2026-11-04 (evening, in the dev tree, nothing committed): three things the product owner saw on the start page. (1) The day track drew one clock per card: a 36-card scan at 21:30 became 36 steps on a `Stepper` that holds seven. `rhythmFor` (D28, amended) now folds cards into moments: cards pushed within `dayRhythm.graceMinutes` (15) of the review or a bundle belong to that point, the rest form interruptions one per run of cards closer together than the grace, each with a `count` and the first card's `title` (`RhythmPointSchema`); the budget's `used` is the number of interruption moments, derived from the rhythm, so the `countPushedSince` port went (both adapters, the store contract). The page says "Avbrott 2 av 3 · 6 kort", and when every point has passed the quiet hours are the current step instead of the review. Unit tests cover the fold and the use case. Not built: the ration itself; nothing holds a push once the budget is spent, so the track can still say "Avbrott 5 av 3" on a day of many scans (every `GET /api/threads` scans), and `GET /api/threads` scanning on every read means a day of development produces interruptions a production day would not. (2) The scrollbar is at the window's edge: the `ChatLayout` is full bleed in `LayoutContent` (`padding={0}`) with the `spacious` density centring the stream and dock to 800 px (D31, amended; `scrollRef` delegation was tried and rejected because Astryx fixes the dock across the sidebar). Cost: the column is 800 px instead of 960, so a card's action row wraps its ⋯ menu to a second line at desktop width; the piles toolbar was never aligned to the column and now sits further left. (3) The product owner found the track non-interactive and "Avbrott 5 av 3" of no value, so the track went (D28, amended again): the digest is one supporting line (review time, threads read, next bundle or next review), the greeting sentence and the budget's promise. The rhythm stays in `GET /api/attention` for the line's times; if nothing else ever reads the points, the fold in `rhythmFor` can be simplified to the fixed points and the count of interruption moments.

Session 24, 2026-11-04 (worktree `tanstack-query`, branch `tanstack-query` from `main` at `5a92b2f`, five commits, not merged): TanStack Query adopted as the one way the browser reads the server, planned in the session's plan file after two explorations (every `api.*` call site, the tooling and test gates), the product owner's four answers (branch after the shell migration landed; devtools in development only; refetch on tab visibility and reconnect, no polling; all screens in one branch) and a fifteen-point review of the plan whose findings shaped the design (the client under the `SessionBoundary` with an `onEnded` callback rather than a module-level client or an effect cleanup; `query-keys.ts` shared by reads and writes; calendar invalidation only for threads with proposals; `run` as a settled promise with a ref as the re-entry guard; the page alert only when a read has nothing to show; `HandledRow` so no hook sits in a loop; the connector removal dropping its own query before leaving). D33 written and accepted. Built: `apps/web/src/api-error.ts`, `query-keys.ts`, `query-policy.ts`, `query-client.ts`, `queries.ts`, `thread-cache.ts`, `mutations.ts`; `Protected` owns one `QueryClient` per confirmed session and the dev-only devtools; the start page reads five queries and announces reminders from whatever list the cache hands it; every card, row, log row and panel action goes through `useThreadAction`, whose thread lands in the cache for every screen; the conversation writes its answer's thread, a draft's and a proposal's into the cache and reads the verbs again after a turn; the calendar page reads one query per week with the previous answer kept on the grid and picks its proposal thread out of the stream's list with `select`; the two calendar dialogs write the cache themselves; administration reads four queries and acts through `useAdminAction`, which waits for the refetch before it toasts. Gone: `clew:refresh`, every page `load()`, the `weeks` map and `onLoadWeek`, `presetCache`, the `version` counter, the `alive` flags, every `api.*` read outside `queries.ts`. Verified: `bun run check` (182 unit, 10 new in `web-thread-cache.test.ts` and `web-query-policy.test.ts`, including the in-flight refetch cancelled by a write), the WCAG suite twice on the worktree build (111 passed, 11 skipped, the same as `main`) plus the new `tests/a11y/cache.spec.ts` (2: the stream and the areas read once across start → kalender → back, so the cache survives a client-side route change; a card view's POST sent once), the production build with no devtools in `dist/assets`, the cruiser and purity unchanged. Licence record in section 3. Not done: the live Chrome pass as Hillevi against Keycloak and Nextcloud (the published port serves `main`'s dev server, so it waits for the merge; then check: a confirm in the calendar panel and the card agreeing without a reload, Inte nu then Ångra from the toast, a 10-second reminder bringing the card back, one POST on `/vy/kort/...` in dev under StrictMode, a tab switch after 15 s refetching once per query, the BFF stopped and a tab switch leaving the stream in place with no alert, logout leaving no `/api` traffic), the manual screen-reader pass, and the three open questions left for later: whether `Protected`'s own `/api/auth/session` read should become a query, whether a `Skeleton` belongs on the first read of the start page, and whether the stream should poll (declined for now, D33 §4).

Session 23, 2026-11-04 (worktree `kalender`, branch `worktree-kalender`, from `main` at `f1bfc36` after the shell migration): the calendar page finished as D32, planned in the session's plan file after three explorations (the web shell and routes, the calendar backend, Astryx's components through the CLI) and the product owner's four answers (calendar only, the shell migration runs elsewhere; every capability; all three reuse targets; FullCalendar's list and month on a phone). Verified: `bun run check` (172 unit, 11 new in `events.test.ts`, a store contract case for a moved proposal), `bun run test:db` (17), the WCAG suite (111, 16 new in `calendar.spec.ts`; `screens.calendar.spec.ts` takes screenshots on `CLEW_SCREENS=1`), screenshots compared with the canvas row in `docs/reviews/2026-11-04-calendar-against-the-canvas.md` (four defects found and fixed there). Merged to `main` as `ae7fa6d`; then the page got the start page's frame (the areas `SideNav` and the attention sentence) and the week grid shows start times only. Not done: the live Chrome pass against Nextcloud, the manual screen-reader pass, the canvas redraw; the new routes add to the debt of folding `/calendar` into capabilities (item 12).

Session 22, 2026-11-04 (branch `astryx-migration`, seven commits, not merged): the full migration of the UI to strict Astryx composition, planned after the review in session 20 and the product owner's three choices (the conversation as a transcript in page flow, the areas as the shell's SideNav, FullCalendar untouched). D31 written and accepted (D13 superseded). Built, one commit per step: (1–2) `components/shell.tsx`, one AppShell for start, calendar and administration (TopNav with identity, Kalender, Administration, the attention sentence and Logga ut; the SideNav each surface brings; `AreasNav` with counts as passive end content and Kommande as a section; the phone drawer and skip link come from AppShell), the start page as `Layout contentWidth 960` with the piles in a `Toolbar` in the column's header and `ChatLayout` docking the row (below 480 px of height everything is in the page flow), `styles.css` down to the imports and two body rules, the `Overlay`, the `ResizeObserver`, the custom ring and the gradient dock deleted; `packages/ui/src/theme.ts` is Neutral with one component override, `scroll-padding-bottom: 240px` on `chat-layout` (D31 item 8), because ChatLayout's sticky dock sets no scroll padding and the focus walk found every stop under it; the a11y helper now treats a frosted (backdrop-filter) sticky layer as covering and finds the dock by walking up from the combobox, and the drawer on a phone is a `dialog` named Navigering that `openNav` opens. (3) `ConversationProvider` owns the conversation; the row (`composer.tsx`) is a ChatComposer with the chips as Tokens in `ChatComposerDrawer`; `conversation.tsx` is the transcript as ChatLayout children in place of the stream, assistant answers through `Markdown`, a proposal to confirm as plain content in a ghost bubble; the title and the way out sit in the column's Toolbar (icon and menu on a phone); a bare Esc closes the conversation, Esc in a dialog or menu is theirs. (4–6) threads are rows (`ThreadRow`, a `List hasDividers edge="flush"` per run) with the title as the button that opens the card; the first in Nu, proposal threads, the piles and the phone get cards; Badges only for status that needs action (deadline today or overdue, påminnelse, att bekräfta, a receipt row not read back); everything else is text or a Token; the digest is a section with the greeting as the h1; groups rank by weight at one size; the log's days are text; the phone's filters and piles are adaptive menus; the TimePicker dialog is a form without an inline style; `WeekAgenda` is a fixed five-column grid of Tokens on the card (`AgendaItem.short`), with real headings only in the phone's calendar; the admin tiles are a reflowing Grid of muted Cards with a dot. (7) the calendar header is a Toolbar and the proposal panel a nested Layout with the reply field in its footer. Verified after every step: `bun run check`, `sh scripts/a11y.sh` (95 cases, with the structural selectors rewritten for the shell, the drawer, the rows and the dock), screenshots of every surface at 1280 and 320 through the harness. Not done: the live Chrome pass (the dev server needs a Keycloak sign-in), the manual screen-reader pass, the canvas redraw (rows named in the plan: Chat-only start page, Mission control, Samtalet får en egen yta, Chips, Svar 4, Kort-handlingar, Att bli klar, Chef-kalender; Clew uppmärksamhet is historical), and the two D30 polish items, which when built follow the rules in the plan: "annan tid" is an exclusive choice (RadioList or two ClickableCards with Boka, not SelectableCard), the plan diff is List rows with a StatusDot, proposals stay muted with the word Förslag. The Astryx gaps met on the way are listed in D31; no gap reports are filed (product owner, 2026-11-04). Plan file for the record: the migration plan lived in the session's plan file; the review in `docs/reviews/2026-11-04-astryx-adoption-against-the-templates.md` is the diagnosis.

Session 21, 2026-11-04 (worktree `design-strommen`, branch `worktree-design-strommen`, nothing committed yet): the design exploration turned into decisions and the first row built. D28 (the stream: Nu/Senare, three piles, the day as one track, snooze as its own column), D29 (Inte nu as a timed snooze that breaks on activity, conditions deferred) and D30 (a proposal thread as an obligation with four steps, replies read back, follow-ups as cards) written after the product owner answered the eight open questions: Nu/Senare over five levels, piles on desktop and phone, times before conditions, steps plus reply reading with a fixed policy, page only (no push or e-mail yet), load bars from the loaded week, two alternatives on the card and more in the panel, attention row first. Built (D28): `snoozed_until` on `thread` (migration `0008`, both adapters, the store contract), `streamGroup`/`dayWord`/`isSnoozed`/`dayRhythm`/`stockholmInstant`/`nextStockholmTime` in the contracts, `threads/domain.ts` with `rhythmFor`, `snoozeThread` sets the next 06:00 and `reopenThread` clears it, the sweep `wakeDue` before every stream read and on the scan plus the early wake when a draft changes (`thread.woken` with `reason`), `attentionFor` returns the rhythm with every card pushed today as an interruption, the start page rebuilt (digest Card with an Astryx Stepper on-track, Nu and Senare as h2 with cards as h3, Till kännedom rows, reason as a Token on desktop and text on a phone, piles as a ToggleButtonGroup on the dock and as wrapping Buttons at the end of the stream on a phone, pile routes `/pagar`, `/inte-nu`, `/hanterat`), the sidebar without Redan hanterat, the deadline badge red only for today and the day word otherwise, "Ta upp nu" in the Inte nu pile. Tests: 10 new unit cases (grouping, day word, wall-clock times, the track, the sweep, the early wake), the store contract covers the column, the a11y fixtures carry a snoozed card and the rhythm, and two helper fixes in `tests/a11y/support.ts` that the new layout exposed: the Tab walk told stops apart by label and position and ended early when a row scrolled into the previous row's spot (now by element identity), and it flagged the toast as obscured by the dock although the toast is in the top layer (now skipped for `:popover-open`). Verified: `bun run check` (129 unit, deps, purity, node), `bun run test:db` (17), `sh scripts/a11y.sh` (92), screenshots of desktop, the Inte nu pile and a phone compared with artboards A and D. Then D29 built: the picker's presets with resolved times (`snoozePresets`: om en timme, efter lunch, i morgon 06:00, nästa vecka, "när jag är tillbaka från <nästa möte>"), the bounded Swedish time grammar (`parseSwedishTime`: om N min/timmar/dagar, efter lunch, i kväll, nästa vecka, i morgon [kl H], <veckodag> [kl H], kl H, HH:MM; 21 cases in `attention.test.ts`), `snoozeThread(persona, id, { preset | text | until })` with a silent reminder workflow per snooze and a 30-day cap, `surface` with `silent` (clears `snoozedUntil`, audits `thread.woken`, skips a snooze woken earlier or moved), `GET /api/snooze/presets`, and `TimePicker` in `card.tsx` (DropdownMenu adaptive with the time as each row's description, "Skriv själv …" opening a Dialog with a TextInput whose parsed time is shown live and whose button names it); Inte nu and Påminn mig share it. The a11y suite goes through the picker (`snoozeFirst` in `support.ts`) and has a test for it. Then D30 built, see the table row: 7 new scenario tests in `samtal.test.ts` (judgement calls, replies read back, a no with two alternatives and the old event removed after the new one, the follow-up policy end to end, undo cancels the workflow, day load, reasons), `confirm` keeps the obligation open and waiting instead of handled (the samtal tests follow), the proposal card has an Astryx Stepper, the unplaced as Tokens, the reply roster (sum, ProgressBar, AvatarGroup, waiting rows with "påmind N gånger") and "Påminn de N nu" (`POST /api/threads/:id/remind-invitees`), and `WeekAgenda` has a ProgressBar per day heading. `expectNoAxeViolations` now measures 2.5.8 with the dock in the flow, because a row that had scrolled under the sticky dock's pile buttons was read as too close to them. Verified at the end: `bun run check` (161 unit), `bun run test:db` (17), `sh scripts/a11y.sh` (95), screenshots of the stream, the piles, the picker, the lens and the waiting obligation. Not done: the live Chrome pass as Hillevi and Hassan (the dev server serves the main tree), a live Temporal run of the `obligation` workflow and a Nextcloud reply read back, the Astryx gap reports (dashed Card, two-line toggle, segmented bar), the SelectableCard pair of artboard G and the structured plan diff of artboard E (both noted in D30 as later polish), the fixture thread `samtal` in `tests/a11y/data.ts` has an unplaced row but no `proposal:` rows.

Session 20, 2026-11-04 (nothing committed, no code): Astryx adoption reviewed against Astryx's own templates (`ai-chat`, `ai-chat-landing`) and layout guides, with the pages rendered through the a11y harness. Verdict in `docs/reviews/2026-11-04-astryx-adoption-against-the-templates.md`: component choice is mostly right, composition is wrong in five places (the floating composer is hand-rolled CSS while `ChatLayout` exists; Badges used for metadata; card soup and nested cards; headings used for size and two leads per region; `WeekAgenda` Grid with `repeat: 'fill'`). Six-step rework order in the review. New rule in `CLAUDE.md`: never rebuild what Astryx ships; search the CLI and templates first, `gap-report` plus a decision before anything custom.

Session 19, 2026-11-04: the calendar page needs to become a full calendar without a hand-rolled component. Open source survey in `docs/reviews/2026-11-04-calendar-component-alternatives.md`: Astryx 0.7.7 has no event calendar (only the `Calendar` date picker), so a library is an Astryx gap. Recommendation: FullCalendar 7 Standard (MIT; month, week, day, list and interaction are free, Premium is resource views only) through `@fullcalendar/react` 7.1.4, themed with Astryx tokens via v7's class-name props and Astryx components in every slot, proposed as D27 together with a narrowing of the `CLAUDE.md` sentence "the week view is an Astryx Grid agenda, not a time grid" to the start page card. Runner-up Schedule-X (drag is paid, no a11y docs, Preact). Rejected: MUI X Scheduler (Material UI + Emotion, beta), EventCalendar (Svelte runtime, no a11y), ilamy (Tailwind), react-big-calendar (dormant), Toast UI (archived), DayPilot Lite (closed development). FullCalendar's own headless package is undocumented beta; watch it. D27 then accepted the same day; `CLAUDE.md` and the decision table updated. Built on the branch `worktree-kalender-tema`: `@fullcalendar/react` 7.1.4 in `packages/ui`; `packages/ui/src/calendar-theme.ts` (a FullCalendar theme plugin: one class-name prop per element, the shape of the stock themes) and `calendar-theme.css` (every value an Astryx token; the one stylesheet besides `styles.css`); `packages/ui/src/calendar.tsx` exports `EventCalendar` (time grid week, day, month and list views wired, Swedish locale, Stockholm time zone, Monday to Friday, axis 07 to 18 stretched to the week's meetings, `headerToolbar` off, events through Astryx `Text`, day headers as real headings, a visually hidden summary per day, proposals draggable only when the page passes `onMoveProposal`, which the page does not yet). `/kalender` renders it on desktop and keeps `WeekAgenda` on a phone. Verified: typecheck, deps, purity, unit and Node suites green; the 13 calendar cases of the a11y suite green (axe, headings, focus rings, reflow, announcement) run against the worktree with `CLEW_HOST_WORKSPACE=<worktree> docker compose -p clew-kalender-tema -f .devcontainer/compose.yaml --profile a11y run --rm -v <worktree>/node_modules:/workspace/node_modules a11y …`; screenshots compared with the canvas row Chef-kalender. Not built yet: Dag / Vecka / Månad switch, "Ny bokning", the event dialog, the drag write-back and its keyboard path, a11y cases for the new views.

Session 18, 2026-11-04, later: the admin screens reworked after review ("bland, not contained, no navigation"). The shell is Astryx `AppShell` (`variant="section"`, `contentPadding={0}`) with a `TopNav` (Startsidan › Administration, name and unit, Till startsidan, Logga ut) and a `SideNav` (section Anslutningar: Anslutna system selected with the wrench icon, Modeller disabled with a Kommer badge, one sentence on what the surface is for; the shell's drawer on a phone). The page is a transparent `Section` with `padding` 6 (3 on a phone) and `maxWidth` 1216, breadcrumbs, a `PageLead` (h1, one sentence, the primary action at the end), four count tiles as muted `Card`s (Problem turns yellow when non-zero), and the table or the tab panel inside a `Card` with `padding={0}`. Three Astryx containment facts, learned the hard way and worth keeping: a `Layout` nested in a padded container cancels that padding (it is a full-bleed region); a `Section` with `padding={0}` is edge-to-edge by definition and bleeds over the parent's gutters too; a `Card` with `padding={0}` keeps to them, so a bordered box with flush rows is a Card. Table columns have explicit widths (`proportional`, `pixel`), the system name is a `Link` with the id and "inbyggd" under it. Verified in Chrome at 1470 px (gutters 24 px both sides on both pages), `bun run check`, the WCAG suite (92). Commits `f26445b`, `f1fc182`, `5194b47`; `40a691e` removed a worktree gitlink that `git add -A` had swept in and ignores `.claude/worktrees/`. Still open: a canvas row for the admin screens, a manual screen-reader pass.

Session 18, 2026-11-04: D23 finished on top of session 15. Governance in the registry (`Governance`, `inAudience`, `visible`, `allowed`, `listFor`, `capabilitiesFor`; a hidden connector answers "Okänd förmåga." everywhere, `areas`, `scan`, `tools`, the search objects, the typeahead and `GET /api/connectors` filter by it, the stream hides open cards from a hidden connector and keeps handled ones). Bundled connectors get a `connector` row at boot (`bundled`, migration `0007`, `url` and `secret` nullable, `connector_remote` check) so the same audience and switches govern the mocks: Hassan loses Raindance when Mikael limits it to managers. `ConnectorAudience` and the admin contracts in `@clew/contracts`. D25-style administration: `connectors/model.ts` (ports `ConnectorStore`, `ConnectorTx`, `Secrets`, `Transport`, `LiveRegistration`, `Units`), `usecases/admin.ts` (`connectorUsecases(deps)`: list, read, add with the conformance check, update narrowing only with one audit row per changed field, rotateSecret, test, refreshManifest with a diff, remove closing open cards, preview as a role, listUnits; `ForbiddenError` 403 without `admin.connectors`, step-up on every mutation), `adapters/{postgres,memory}.ts` held to `tests/connector-store-contract.ts` on both adapters, `reconcile.ts` (bundled rows, governance applied at boot, a five-second watcher so the CLI and other instances need no restart), `describe.ts`, `packages/connectors/src/check.ts` (the checker the CLI and "Testa anslutning" share; `scripts/connector-check.ts` is a thin wrapper), `routes/admin.ts` under `/api/admin`, `admin: true` on the session response. Screens in `apps/web/src/pages/admin/` (list with Table or List rows, four-step add dialog, detail with Översikt, Förmågor, Områden, Vem ser det with the structural preview, Historik; a phone gets the sections as a menu because five tabs do not fit 320 px), entry "Administration" in the start header, `NotAdmin` otherwise; 16 Astryx components re-exported in `packages/ui`. Session 25, 2026-11-04 (worktree `worktree-fraga-d26`, seven commits, not merged): D26 built end to end in six phases, then the core connector renamed from `startsidan` to `clew` because the product name is UI text, not an identifier (`clew.search`, `clew.openSource`, `clew.fetchSource`, `clew.cardFromAnswer`, question threads and answer cards with `connector: 'clew'`; migration 0010 rewrites the rows written before the rename; the source shown to people stays "Startsidan"). The phases, each with `bun run check` green and the WCAG suite green (119 passed, 11 skipped as before): A the contract, the composite search and the router's catch-all with a parallel fan-out under a budget; B the assistant's document rules, citations, "Steg", follow-ups and the document chip; C the sources view, citations in the transcript and "Gör till ett kort" with eight new accessibility tests; D the card from an answer in the threads module; E delegation by token exchange, verified against the dev Keycloak; F `connectors/eneo`. Learned on the way: TypeBox's `Value.Check` knows no `date-time` format until one is registered (now done once in `@clew/contracts`); Vite listens on 5173 inside the container (published as 3020) and a second dev stack cannot start beside the first, so the exchange check ran the login through the running BFF and the exchange in-process (`BFF_URL`); running the WCAG suite from a worktree needs `COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=clew` so the Playwright service finds the shared `node_modules` volume.

Session 15, 2026-11-04 (in parallel with session 14): testing strategy settled and the first seam cut (D25). Use cases are factories over ports: `apps/server/src/threads/model.ts` (`ThreadStore`, `ThreadTx`, `ReminderTimers`), `threads/usecases/cards.ts` as `threadUsecases(deps)` and `reminders.ts` as `reminderUsecases(deps)` with `clock` and `ids` injected, `threads/adapters/postgres.ts` and `temporal.ts` (live), `threads/adapters/memory.ts` (snapshot transactions, `audit` and `jobs` exposed), and `apps/server/src/composition.ts` as the one composition root that routes, the scheduler and the assistant import from. `actorOf` moved to `@clew/connectors` (re-exported from the registry). `readFigures` takes `now`. Tests: `tests/store-contract.ts` run by `tests/unit/memory-store.test.ts` and `tests/db/postgres-store.test.ts` (6 cases, both green), `tests/unit/threads.test.ts` (17 scenarios: scan, refresh with selection kept, concurrent scans, act with receipt and job, read action, undo in and out of the window, close receipt window, dismiss/snooze/reopen, rollback of a failed mutation leaves no audit row, one audit row per mutation, attention, areas, reminders), `tests/unit/proposals.property.test.ts` (fast-check 4.10.2, MIT). Guards: `usecases-reach-stores-through-ports` (error for `threads/`, warning for `modules/*/usecases` until `samtal.ts` is ported), `unit-tests-stay-fast`, `scripts/purity-check.ts` as `bun run purity` inside `bun run check`; the cruiser cannot see `import type` under TypeScript 7, so a test takes store types from `packages/db/src/schema.ts` (`remote-connector.test.ts` changed accordingly). Two audit gaps closed: `thread.refreshed` (only when the draft changed) and `thread.receipt_final` (actor `system:scheduler`). Docs: D25, `docs/architecture/testing.md` (where a test goes, kinds, the contract, what check never needs, porting order), `backend-structure.md` (layout, table, rule 8, debt), `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/README.md`. Verified: `bun run check` green in the container (71 unit tests, 8 cruiser warnings, 0 errors), `bun run test:db` green, `bun scripts/modules-check.ts` green against the running stack. Then the calendar and the mocks ported the same way, and the warning became an error: `modules/calendar/model.ts` (`ProposalRecord`, `ProposalStore`, `ProposalTx`, `CalendarBinder`), `usecases/samtal.ts` as `calendarUsecases(deps)` (reads, status, draw, replan, confirm, undo, closeUndoWindow, dismiss, weekOverview; `reads.ts` keeps the pure projections), `adapters/{postgres,memory}.ts`, `packages/calendar/src/memory.ts` (`memoryCalendar`, the `CalendarProvider` port in memory, exported as `@clew/calendar/memory`), `modules/mocks/{model.ts,usecases.ts,adapters/postgres.ts}`; `composition.ts` became `composition/` (`ambient.ts`, `calendar.ts` before the registry, `index.ts`) because the registry builds the calendar connector over the calendar use cases (`calendarConnector(calendar)`) and the stream is built over the registry; routes, scheduler and assistant take `calendar` and `mocks` from there. `tests/proposal-store-contract.ts` (4 cases, memory and Postgres), `tests/unit/samtal.test.ts` (12 scenarios: draw per member, held members left out, planning around busy time, unreadable calendar refused, replan supersedes, confirm with events, re-validation, lost reply, undo in and out of the window, final by timer, rollback, dismiss, week and today). `usecases-reach-stores-through-ports` is an error for every `usecases` folder and also forbids adapters; the purity check covers them all. Verified: `bun run check` green (86 unit tests, no cruiser violations), `bun run test:db` green (10), `bun scripts/modules-check.ts` green against the running stack, and confirm plus undo run live against Nextcloud through `composition/calendar.ts` (event created, then removed). `bun scripts/auth-dev-check.ts` cannot find the samtal thread any more: `/api/threads` returns the 50 newest threads and the dev database now holds more than 50 question and mock threads newer than it (a pre-existing cap in `listThreads`, not a regression); clear old question threads or raise the cap before relying on that script. Committed as `cd3b041`. Then the rest ported the same way: `reminders.surface` (the Temporal activity's body, over a new `ThreadTx.lockThread` with a contract case; `temporal/activities.ts` is one line over the composition root and `workflows.ts` takes `ReminderInput` from `threads/model.ts`), `scheduler/` (`model.ts` with `JobQueue` and `People`, `usecases.ts` with `createScheduler`, `adapters/postgres.ts`; `main.ts` calls `scheduler.start()`), the assistant as `assistantUsecases(deps)` over `ThreadStore` and a `ModelProvider` port (`assistant/ports.ts`; `adapters/provider.ts` is the old `status.ts` plus `createProvider(env)` with no default, `adapters/memory.ts` for tests; `chips`, `objects`, `completions`, `fixed` take `deps`; `parseVerb(text, thread, today)`), and the purity guard now also refuses `today()` and `isPast()` from `@clew/calendar/dates` in server code. The cruiser rule covers `threads/`, `modules/*`, `scheduler/` and `assistant/` except `adapters/` and `connector.ts`; the Temporal adapter names the workflow instead of typing it so no type-only import closes a cycle the cruiser cannot see through. Tests: `tests/unit/scheduler.test.ts` (3), `tests/unit/conversation.test.ts` (8: router answers and audit, fixed questions, completions and objects, chip verbs, drafts, replies, replan through the conversation, the model path and its fallback), reminder surfacing in `threads.test.ts`, a lock case in the store contract. `bun run check` green (99 unit tests), `bun run test:db` green (11), smoke script green. Next: the people connector reads the grant tables directly (a port when SCIM lands, D5); `dates.ts` `today()`/`isPast()` stay for the browser.
